import pytest

from backend.agents.cybersecurity_intelligence_agent import (
    CybersecurityIntelligenceAgent,
)
from backend.cybersecurity.intelligence_models import (
    CybersecuritySignal,
)
from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_prioritises_critical_signal():
    agent = CybersecurityIntelligenceAgent()

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="test-user",
        request="Assess current cybersecurity posture.",
        metadata={
            "cybersecurity_signals": [
                CybersecuritySignal(
                    signal_id="sig-1",
                    category="vulnerability",
                    title="Critical dependency vulnerability",
                    severity="critical",
                    confidence=0.98,
                    source="security-scanner",
                    affected_area="backend",
                    status="open",
                ),
                CybersecuritySignal(
                    signal_id="sig-2",
                    category="event",
                    title="Low severity event",
                    severity="low",
                    confidence=0.90,
                    source="security-events",
                    status="open",
                ),
            ]
        },
    )

    result = await agent.execute(context)

    assert result.success is True
    assert result.output["posture"] == "CRITICAL ATTENTION REQUIRED"
    assert result.output["critical_count"] == 1
    assert result.output["priority_signals"][0]["signal_id"] == "sig-1"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_requires_security_signals():
    agent = CybersecurityIntelligenceAgent()

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="test-user",
        request="Assess security.",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(context)

    assert result.success is False
    assert "cybersecurity_signals" in result.error

"""
Tests for the external AI Runtime composition.

These tests verify that the application composition root:

- creates the approved capability executors;
- registers every supported external capability;
- preserves dependency injection;
- creates the CapabilityGateway with the supplied governance object;
- does not execute capabilities during composition.

The tests deliberately use mocks for externally constructed agents
and providers. The composition layer receives those dependencies;
it is not responsible for constructing their internal dependencies.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any
from unittest.mock import Mock

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceAgent,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.flashcard_generation_executor import (
    FlashcardGenerationExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.gateway import (
    CapabilityGateway,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.listening_proposal_executor import (
    ListeningProposalExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.live_intelligence_executor import (
    LiveIntelligenceExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.podcast_discovery_executor import (
    PodcastDiscoveryExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.podcast_learning_challenge_executor import (
    PodcastLearningChallengeExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)

from src.ai_runtime.composition import (
    create_capability_executors,
    create_capability_gateway,
    create_capability_registry,
    create_external_runtime_gateway,
)

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.agent import (
    PodcastDiscoveryAgent,
)

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.providers import (
    PodcastProvider,
)


def make_live_intelligence_agent() -> LiveIntelligenceAgent:
    """
    Create a mocked Live Intelligence agent.

    The real LiveIntelligenceAgent requires its own search provider
    and processor dependencies. Those dependencies are outside the
    responsibility of the external Runtime composition tests.

    The composition root only needs an already-constructed agent.
    """

    return Mock(
        spec=LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    )


def make_podcast_discovery_agent() -> PodcastDiscoveryAgent:
    """
    Create a mocked Podcast Discovery agent.

    Podcast Discovery behaviour is tested separately. These tests
    verify only that the existing agent is injected into the
    Runtime executor.
    """

    return Mock(
        spec=PodcastDiscoveryAgent,
    )


def make_podcast_provider() -> PodcastProvider:
    """
    Create a mocked Podcast provider.

    Provider behaviour is tested separately. The composition layer
    only needs to receive a configured provider dependency.
    """

    return Mock(
        spec=PodcastProvider,
    )


def make_podcast_providers() -> list[PodcastProvider]:
    """
    Return the Podcast providers supplied to the composition root.
    """

    return [
        make_podcast_provider(),
    ]


def make_governance() -> Any:
    """
    Create a minimal governance test double.

    Governance is injected into the external Runtime. The composition
    module must not create a competing governance implementation.
    """

    class FakeGovernance:
        pass

    return FakeGovernance()


def test_create_capability_executors_creates_all_supported_executors():
    """
    Verify that the composition root creates every currently
    supported external Runtime capability executor.
    """

    live_agent = make_live_intelligence_agent()
    podcast_agent = make_podcast_discovery_agent()
    providers = make_podcast_providers()

    executors = create_capability_executors(
        live_intelligence_agent=live_agent,
        podcast_discovery_agent=podcast_agent,
        podcast_providers=providers,
    )

    assert len(executors) == 5

    assert all(
        isinstance(
            executor,
            CapabilityExecutor,
        )
        for executor in executors
    )

    assert any(
        isinstance(
            executor,
            LiveIntelligenceExecutor,
        )
        for executor in executors
    )

    assert any(
        isinstance(
            executor,
            PodcastDiscoveryExecutor,
        )
        for executor in executors
    )

    assert any(
        isinstance(
            executor,
            PodcastLearningChallengeExecutor,
        )
        for executor in executors
    )

    assert any(
        isinstance(
            executor,
            ListeningProposalExecutor,
        )
        for executor in executors
    )

    assert any(
        isinstance(
            executor,
            FlashcardGenerationExecutor,
        )
        for executor in executors
    )


def test_create_capability_executors_preserves_injected_agents():
    """
    Verify that the composition root passes the supplied agents
    into the appropriate executors.
    """

    live_agent = make_live_intelligence_agent()
    podcast_agent = make_podcast_discovery_agent()

    executors = create_capability_executors(
        live_intelligence_agent=live_agent,
        podcast_discovery_agent=podcast_agent,
        podcast_providers=make_podcast_providers(),
    )

    live_executor = next(
        executor
        for executor in executors
        if isinstance(
            executor,
            LiveIntelligenceExecutor,
        )
    )

    podcast_executor = next(
        executor
        for executor in executors
        if isinstance(
            executor,
            PodcastDiscoveryExecutor,
        )
    )

    assert live_executor._agent is live_agent
    assert podcast_executor._agent is podcast_agent


def test_create_capability_registry_registers_all_capabilities():
    """
    Verify that all currently supported external Runtime
    capabilities are registered.
    """

    registry = create_capability_registry(
        live_intelligence_agent=(
            make_live_intelligence_agent()
        ),
        podcast_discovery_agent=(
            make_podcast_discovery_agent()
        ),
        podcast_providers=(
            make_podcast_providers()
        ),
    )

    assert isinstance(
        registry,
        CapabilityRegistry,
    )

    assert registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
    )

    assert registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.PODCAST_DISCOVERY,
    )

    assert registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION,
    )

    assert registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.PODCAST_LISTENING_PROPOSAL,
    )

    assert registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.FLASHCARD_GENERATION,
    )


def test_create_capability_registry_resolves_expected_executors():
    """
    Verify that registered capabilities resolve to the correct
    executor implementations.
    """

    registry = create_capability_registry(
        live_intelligence_agent=(
            make_live_intelligence_agent()
        ),
        podcast_discovery_agent=(
            make_podcast_discovery_agent()
        ),
        podcast_providers=(
            make_podcast_providers()
        ),
    )

    assert isinstance(
        registry.resolve(
            CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        ),
        LiveIntelligenceExecutor,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        registry.resolve(
            CapabilityType.PODCAST_DISCOVERY,
        ),
        PodcastDiscoveryExecutor,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        registry.resolve(
            CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION,
        ),
        PodcastLearningChallengeExecutor,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        registry.resolve(
            CapabilityType.PODCAST_LISTENING_PROPOSAL,
        ),
        ListeningProposalExecutor,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        registry.resolve(
            CapabilityType.FLASHCARD_GENERATION,
        ),
        FlashcardGenerationExecutor,
    )


def test_create_capability_gateway_preserves_registry_and_governance():
    """
    Verify that the gateway receives the exact registry and
    governance dependencies supplied by the composition layer.
    """

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()
    governance = make_governance()

    gateway = create_capability_gateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        gateway,
        CapabilityGateway,
    )

    assert gateway._registry is registry
    assert gateway._governance is governance


def test_create_external_runtime_gateway_creates_configured_gateway():
    """
    Verify the complete external Runtime composition path.
    """

    governance = make_governance()

    gateway = create_external_runtime_gateway(
        live_intelligence_agent=(
            make_live_intelligence_agent()
        ),
        podcast_discovery_agent=(
            make_podcast_discovery_agent()
        ),
        podcast_providers=(
            make_podcast_providers()
        ),
        governance=governance,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        gateway,
        CapabilityGateway,
    )

    assert gateway._governance is governance

    assert gateway._registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
    )

    assert gateway._registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.PODCAST_DISCOVERY,
    )

    assert gateway._registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION,
    )

    assert gateway._registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.PODCAST_LISTENING_PROPOSAL,
    )

    assert gateway._registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.FLASHCARD_GENERATION,
    )


def test_composition_does_not_execute_capabilities():
    """
    Verify that composition only constructs dependencies.

    Creating the Runtime gateway must not execute a capability,
    contact a provider, generate a learning challenge, create
    a listening proposal, or generate flashcards.
    """

    governance = make_governance()

    gateway = create_external_runtime_gateway(
        live_intelligence_agent=(
            make_live_intelligence_agent()
        ),
        podcast_discovery_agent=(
            make_podcast_discovery_agent()
        ),
        podcast_providers=(
            make_podcast_providers()
        ),
        governance=governance,
    )

    assert gateway is not None

    capabilities = gateway._registry.capabilities()

    assert len(capabilities) == 5

    assert (
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.PODCAST_DISCOVERY
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.PODCAST_LISTENING_PROPOSAL
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.FLASHCARD_GENERATION
        in capabilities
    )

"""
Tests for Parallel Podcast Provider.

Validates:

PodcastDiscoveryExecutor
        |
        v
PodcastProvider Contract
        |
        v
ParallelPodcastProvider

These tests do not call:

- Parallel API
- external providers
- Gemini
- Google Cloud
"""


from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import Mock

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    LearningContext,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
)

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.parallel import (
    ParallelPodcastProvider,
)


def create_test_intent() -> PodcastSearchIntent:
    """
    Create deterministic provider-neutral search intent.
    """

    return PodcastSearchIntent(
        user_id="test-user",
        learning_context=LearningContext(
            world_id="hackathon",
            target_language="fr",
            learner_level="B2",
        ),
        topics=(
            "French learning",
        ),
    )


def test_parallel_provider_without_client_returns_empty_results():
    """
    Sprint 0 behaviour.

    Without an injected client:

    - no external calls;
    - deterministic response.
    """

    provider = ParallelPodcastProvider()

    results = provider.search(
        create_test_intent(),
    )

    assert results == []


def test_parallel_provider_exposes_provider_id():
    """
    Provider exposes stable identifier.
    """

    provider = ParallelPodcastProvider()

    assert provider.provider_id == "parallel"


def test_parallel_provider_uses_injected_client():
    """
    Provider must use dependency injection.

    It must not construct external clients itself.
    """

    client = Mock()

    client.search.return_value = []

    provider = ParallelPodcastProvider(
        client=client,
    )

    provider.search(
        create_test_intent(),
    )

    client.search.assert_called_once()


def test_parallel_provider_maps_results():
    """
    External provider responses must be converted
    into provider-neutral PodcastSearchResult objects.
    """

    client = Mock()

    client.search.return_value = [
        {
            "id": "episode-001",
            "title": "French Podcast",
            "description": "Learning French",
            "publisher": "Parallel",
            "languages": [
                "fr",
            ],
            "url": (
                "https://example.com/podcast"
            ),
        }
    ]

    provider = ParallelPodcastProvider(
        client=client,
    )

    results = provider.search(
        create_test_intent(),
    )

    assert len(results) == 1

    result = results[0]

    assert result.title == "French Podcast"
    assert result.publisher == "Parallel"
    assert result.url.startswith(
        "https://"
    )

"""
Runtime composition Parallel provider integration tests.

Validates:

Application Composition
        |
        v
CapabilityRegistry
        |
        v
PodcastDiscoveryExecutor
        |
        v
PodcastRecommendationService
        |
        v
ParallelPodcastProvider


Does not call:

- Parallel API
- external services
- Gemini
- Google Cloud

The Runtime currently composes five supported capability
executors, including Flashcard Generation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import Mock

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.flashcard_generation_executor import (
    FlashcardGenerationExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.listening_proposal_executor import (
    ListeningProposalExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.live_intelligence_executor import (
    LiveIntelligenceExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.podcast_discovery_executor import (
    PodcastDiscoveryExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.podcast_learning_challenge_executor import (
    PodcastLearningChallengeExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.composition import (
    create_capability_executors,
)

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.parallel import (
    ParallelPodcastProvider,
)


class FakeLiveIntelligenceAgent:
    """
    Controlled Live Intelligence dependency.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return {
            "status": "completed",
        }


class FakePodcastDiscoveryAgent:
    """
    Controlled Podcast Discovery dependency.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return {
            "status": "completed",
        }


def test_runtime_composition_accepts_parallel_provider():
    """
    Composition root must accept Parallel provider
    through the existing PodcastProvider boundary.

    It must not know Parallel implementation details.

    The Runtime currently creates five supported capability
    executors.
    """

    provider = ParallelPodcastProvider()

    executors = create_capability_executors(
        live_intelligence_agent=(
            FakeLiveIntelligenceAgent()
        ),
        podcast_discovery_agent=(
            FakePodcastDiscoveryAgent()
        ),
        podcast_providers=[
            provider,
        ],
    )

    assert len(executors) == 5

    assert any(
        isinstance(
            executor,
            LiveIntelligenceExecutor,
        )
        for executor in executors
    )

    assert any(
        isinstance(
            executor,
            PodcastDiscoveryExecutor,
        )
        for executor in executors
    )

    assert any(
        isinstance(
            executor,
            PodcastLearningChallengeExecutor,
        )
        for executor in executors
    )

    assert any(
        isinstance(
            executor,
            ListeningProposalExecutor,
        )
        for executor in executors
    )

    assert any(
        isinstance(
            executor,
            FlashcardGenerationExecutor,
        )
        for executor in executors
    )


def test_parallel_provider_is_injected_without_runtime_changes():
    """
    Verify provider injection remains explicit.

    Runtime composition receives providers;
    it does not create them.

    The supplied Parallel provider must reach the existing
    PodcastRecommendationService through the
    PodcastDiscoveryExecutor.

    The test intentionally verifies the existing ownership
    boundary rather than requiring PodcastDiscoveryExecutor
    to expose provider state directly.
    """

    provider = ParallelPodcastProvider(
        client=Mock(),
    )

    executors = create_capability_executors(
        live_intelligence_agent=(
            FakeLiveIntelligenceAgent()
        ),
        podcast_discovery_agent=(
            FakePodcastDiscoveryAgent()
        ),
        podcast_providers=[
            provider,
        ],
    )

    podcast_executor = next(
        executor
        for executor in executors
        if isinstance(
            executor,
            PodcastDiscoveryExecutor,
        )
    )

    assert podcast_executor is not None

    recommendation_service = (
        podcast_executor._recommendation_service
    )

    assert recommendation_service is not None

    assert (
        provider
        in recommendation_service._providers
    )

"""
API test composition fixtures.

Provides controlled application dependencies
for API tests.

Tests must not:

- create real providers;
- call external services;
- execute real Runtime infrastructure.

Instead they inject approved test doubles.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.gateway import (
    CapabilityGateway,
)

from backend.services.external_runtime_service import (
    ExternalRuntimeService,
)

from backend.api.routes import (
    configure_external_runtime_service,
)


@pytest.fixture
def mock_external_runtime_service():
    """
    Create a controlled ExternalRuntimeService
    for API tests.
    """

    gateway = Mock(
        spec=CapabilityGateway,
    )

    service = ExternalRuntimeService(
        gateway=gateway,
    )

    service.execute = AsyncMock()

    configure_external_runtime_service(
        service,
    )

    return service

import pytest

from httpx import ASGITransport, AsyncClient

from backend.api.main import app
from backend.api import routes


class FakeCompanionService:
    """
    Test double for the application service.

    This test deliberately does not invoke Parallel.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        character,
    ):
        self.character = character
        self.received_context = None
        self.received_use_research = None

    async def interact(
        self,
        context,
        use_research=False,
    ):
        self.received_context = context
        self.received_use_research = (
            use_research
        )

        return {
            "character": self.character.name,
            "conversation": {
                "character": self.character.name,
                "message": (
                    f"{self.character.name}: "
                    f"Test response"
                ),
                "intelligence_context": (
                    "TEST INTELLIGENCE"
                    if use_research
                    else None
                ),
            },
            "research": (
                {
                    "query": context.request,
                    "test": True,
                }
                if use_research
                else None
            ),
            "quality": {
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
                "findings": [],
                "recommendations": [],
            },
            "memory_count": 0,
        }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_chat_passes_research_false(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Verify /chat passes research=False
    to CompanionService.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory(
        character,
    ):
        service = FakeCompanionService(
            character
        )

        captured["service"] = service

        return service

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "create_companion_service",
        fake_factory,
    )

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.post(
            "/chat",
            json={
                "user_id": "api-test-user",
                "character": "yeti",
                "message": (
                    "Explain artificial intelligence"
                ),
                "research": False,
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    data = response.json()

    service = captured["service"]

    assert (
        service.received_use_research
        is False
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.user_id
        == "api-test-user"
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.request
        == "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert data["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert data["research"] is None

    assert (
        data["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        is None
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_chat_passes_research_true(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Verify /chat passes research=True
    to CompanionService.

    This is an API contract test and therefore
    intentionally does not call Parallel.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory(
        character,
    ):
        service = FakeCompanionService(
            character
        )

        captured["service"] = service

        return service

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "create_companion_service",
        fake_factory,
    )

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.post(
            "/chat",
            json={
                "user_id": "api-test-user",
                "character": "yeti",
                "message": (
                    "What are the latest "
                    "European AI regulation "
                    "developments?"
                ),
                "research": True,
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    data = response.json()

    service = captured["service"]

    assert (
        service.received_use_research
        is True
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.user_id
        == "api-test-user"
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.request
        == (
            "What are the latest "
            "European AI regulation "
            "developments?"
        )
    )

    assert data["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert data["research"] is not None

    assert (
        data["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        == "TEST INTELLIGENCE"
    )

"""
EasyChatBot Operations Center HTTP bootstrap tests.

Validates that the existing FastAPI application bootstrap
registers the Operations Center router.

This test intentionally verifies the existing application
bootstrap boundary. It does not create a second FastAPI app
or a second Runtime.
"""

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from backend.api.main import create_app


def test_operations_center_dashboard_route_is_registered() -> None:
    """
    The existing FastAPI application must expose the
    Operations Center dashboard endpoint.
    """

    app = create_app()

    client = TestClient(
        app,
        raise_server_exceptions=False,
    )

    response = client.get(

    assert events_response.status_code == 405

"""
Tests for the Podcast Discovery API endpoint.

The API endpoint must remain a thin transport boundary.

Architecture:

HTTP
 |
 v
API Schema
 |
 v
ExternalRuntimeService
 |
 v
CapabilityGateway
 |
 v
PodcastDiscoveryExecutor
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock, patch

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from backend.api.main import app

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


client = TestClient(app)


def test_podcast_discovery_endpoint_exists():
    """
    Verify that the Podcast Discovery endpoint exists.
    """

    response = client.post(
        "/capabilities/podcast/discover",
        json={
            "query": "French learning podcast",
            "target_language": "fr",
            "learner_level": "B2",
        },
    )

    assert response.status_code != 404


def test_podcast_discovery_endpoint_delegates_to_runtime_service():
    """
    Verify that the endpoint delegates execution to the
    ExternalRuntimeService boundary.
    """

    expected_result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="podcast-api-test-001",
        capability=CapabilityType.PODCAST_DISCOVERY,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
        output={
            "podcast": "French Podcast",
        },
    )

    service = Mock()

    service.execute = AsyncMock(
        return_value=expected_result,
    )

    with patch(
        "backend.api.routes.create_external_runtime_service",
        return_value=service,
    ):
        response = client.post(
            "/capabilities/podcast/discover",
            json={
                "query": "French learning podcast",
                "target_language": "fr",
                "learner_level": "B2",
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    service.execute.assert_awaited_once()


def test_podcast_discovery_endpoint_rejects_missing_query():
    """
    Verify that missing query input is rejected.
    """

    response = client.post(
        "/capabilities/podcast/discover",
        json={
            "target_language": "fr",
            "learner_level": "B2",
        },
    )

    assert response.status_code in (
        400,
        422,
    )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\api\test_routes.py

"""
API route tests.

These tests verify the HTTP/API boundary.

The tests deliberately replace the application service
with deterministic fakes so they never call:

- OpenAI
- Parallel
- external APIs
- real provider infrastructure

Architecture under test:

HTTP
|
v
API Route
|
v
CompanionService
|
v
Application Result
"""

import pytest

from httpx import (
    ASGITransport,
    AsyncClient,
)

from backend.api.main import (
    app,
)

from backend.api import (
    routes,
)


class FakeCompanionService:
    """
    Deterministic CompanionService test double.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        character,
    ) -> None:
        self.character = character
        self.received_context = None
        self.received_use_research = None

    async def interact(
        self,
        context,
        use_research=False,
    ):
        """
        Capture the application request and return
        a deterministic API result.
        """

        self.received_context = context

        self.received_use_research = (
            use_research
        )

        return {
            "character": self.character.name,

            "conversation": {
                "character": self.character.name,
                "message": (
                    f"{self.character.name}: "
                    "Test response"
                ),
                "intelligence_context": (
                    "TEST INTELLIGENCE"
                    if use_research
                    else None
                ),
            },

            "research": (
                {
                    "query": context.request,
                    "test": True,
                }
                if use_research
                else None
            ),

            "quality": {
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
                "findings": [],
                "recommendations": [],
            },

            "memory_count": 0,
        }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_chat_passes_research_false(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    /chat should pass research=False to the
    CompanionService.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory(
        character,
    ):
        service = FakeCompanionService(
            character
        )

        captured["service"] = service

        return service

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "create_companion_service",
        fake_factory,
    )

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.post(
            "/chat",
            json={
                "user_id": "api-test-user",
                "character": "yeti",
                "message": (
                    "Explain artificial intelligence"
                ),
                "research": False,
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    data = response.json()

    service = captured["service"]

    assert (
        service.received_use_research
        is False
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.user_id
        == "api-test-user"
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.request
        == "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert data["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert data["research"] is None

    assert (
        data["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        is None
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_chat_passes_research_true(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    /chat should pass research=True to the
    CompanionService.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory(
        character,
    ):
        service = FakeCompanionService(
            character
        )

        captured["service"] = service

        return service

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "create_companion_service",
        fake_factory,
    )

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.post(
            "/chat",
            json={
                "user_id": "api-test-user",
                "character": "yeti",
                "message": (
                    "What are the latest "
                    "European AI regulation "
                    "developments?"
                ),
                "research": True,
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    data = response.json()

    service = captured["service"]

    assert (
        service.received_use_research
        is True
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.user_id
        == "api-test-user"
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.request
        == (
            "What are the latest "
            "European AI regulation "
            "developments?"
        )
    )

    assert data["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert data["research"] is not None

    assert (
        data["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        == "TEST INTELLIGENCE"
    )

    )

    async with AsyncClient(

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_get_characters_returns_character_data():
    """
    /characters should expose the approved character
    registry through the API.
    """

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.get(
            "/characters"
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    data = response.json()

    assert "characters" in data

    assert isinstance(
        data["characters"],
        list,
    )

    assert len(
        data["characters"]
    ) > 0

    yeti = next(
        character
        for character in data["characters"]
        if character["id"] == "yeti"
    )

    assert yeti["name"] == "Yeti"

    assert "description" in yeti

    assert "personality" in yeti

    assert "learning_style" in yeti

        "/chat",

            "character": "yeti",
            "message": "Hello",
            "research": False,

    assert response.status_code in (

    """
    Verify API routes remain application-facing.

    """
    Verify the API composition helper can create the
    ExternalRuntimeService boundary.
    """

    from backend.api.routes import (
        create_external_runtime_service,
    )

    with patch(
        "backend.api.routes.create_external_runtime_gateway",
    ) as gateway_factory:

        gateway = Mock()

        gateway_factory.return_value = gateway

        service = (
            create_external_runtime_service()
        )

        assert service is not None

        assert service._gateway is gateway

application services and their Runtime dependencies.

        LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    )

    return Mock(
        spec=LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    )

        PodcastProvider,

    return Mock(
        spec=PodcastProvider,

    external Runtime.
    """

    class FakeGovernance:
        pass

    return FakeGovernance()


def test_application_factory_creates_external_runtime_service():

        service,
        ExternalRuntimeService,

        service._gateway,
        CapabilityGateway,
    )


def test_application_factory_uses_runtime_composition():

        podcast_discovery_agent=(

            make_podcast_provider(),
        ],
        governance=make_governance(),
    )

    service = factory.create_external_runtime_service(
        gateway=gateway,
    )

    assert service._gateway is gateway

"""
Tests for application composition of the external AI Runtime.

These tests verify that the application composition layer:

- creates the external Runtime gateway;
- injects the gateway into ExternalRuntimeService;
- preserves dependency injection;
- does not alter the existing CompanionService flow.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import Mock

from backend.services.external_runtime_service import (
    ExternalRuntimeService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.gateway import (
    CapabilityGateway,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.composition import (
    create_external_runtime_gateway,
)


def make_live_intelligence_agent():
    """
    Create a mocked Live Intelligence agent dependency.
    """

    from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
        LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    )

    return Mock(
        spec=LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    )


def make_podcast_discovery_agent():
    """
    Create a mocked Podcast Discovery agent dependency.
    """

    from src.ai_runtime.podcast.agent import (
        PodcastDiscoveryAgent,
    )

    return Mock(
        spec=PodcastDiscoveryAgent,
    )


def make_podcast_provider():
    """
    Create a mocked Podcast provider dependency.
    """

    from src.ai_runtime.podcast.providers import (
        PodcastProvider,
    )

    return Mock(
        spec=PodcastProvider,
    )


def make_governance():
    """
    Create a minimal governance dependency.

    Governance is supplied by the application composition layer.
    """

    class FakeGovernance:
        pass

    return FakeGovernance()


def test_application_can_create_external_runtime_gateway():
    """
    Verify that application composition can construct the external
    Runtime gateway from injected dependencies.
    """

    gateway = create_external_runtime_gateway(
        live_intelligence_agent=(
            make_live_intelligence_agent()
        ),
        podcast_discovery_agent=(
            make_podcast_discovery_agent()
        ),
        podcast_providers=[
            make_podcast_provider(),
        ],
        governance=make_governance(),
    )

    assert isinstance(
        gateway,
        CapabilityGateway,
    )

    assert gateway._registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
    )

    assert gateway._registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.PODCAST_DISCOVERY,
    )

    assert gateway._registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION,
    )

    assert gateway._registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.PODCAST_LISTENING_PROPOSAL,
    )


def test_application_can_inject_gateway_into_external_runtime_service():
    """
    Verify that the configured external Runtime gateway can be
    injected into the application service.
    """

    gateway = create_external_runtime_gateway(
        live_intelligence_agent=(
            make_live_intelligence_agent()
        ),
        podcast_discovery_agent=(
            make_podcast_discovery_agent()
        ),
        podcast_providers=[
            make_podcast_provider(),
        ],
        governance=make_governance(),
    )

    service = ExternalRuntimeService(
        gateway=gateway,
    )

    assert service._gateway is gateway

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\application\test_external_runtime_service.py

"""
Tests for the External Runtime application service.

The production service does not exist yet.

These tests define the required application-layer contract before
implementation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.gateway import (
    CapabilityGateway,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)

from backend.services.external_runtime_service import (
    ExternalRuntimeService,
)


def make_request() -> CapabilityRequest:
    """
    Create a minimal external Runtime capability request.
    """

    return CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.PODCAST_DISCOVERY,
        request_id="application-runtime-test-001",
        payload={
            "query": "French learning podcast",
        },
        requested_by="learning_application",
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_external_runtime_service_delegates_to_gateway():
    """
    Verify that the application service delegates execution to
    the injected CapabilityGateway.
    """

    gateway = Mock(
        spec=CapabilityGateway,
    )

    expected_result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="application-runtime-test-001",
        capability=CapabilityType.PODCAST_DISCOVERY,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
        output={
            "ok": True,
        },
    )

    gateway.execute = AsyncMock(
        return_value=expected_result,
    )

    service = ExternalRuntimeService(
        gateway=gateway,
    )

    request = make_request()

    result = await service.execute(
        request,
    )

    assert result is expected_result

    gateway.execute.assert_awaited_once_with(
        request,
    )


def test_external_runtime_service_requires_gateway():
    """
    Verify that the application service rejects a missing gateway.
    """

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="CapabilityGateway cannot be None",
    ):
        ExternalRuntimeService(
            gateway=None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_external_runtime_service_rejects_missing_request():
    """
    Verify that the application service rejects a missing request
    before attempting Runtime execution.
    """

    gateway = Mock(
        spec=CapabilityGateway,
    )

    gateway.execute = AsyncMock()

    service = ExternalRuntimeService(
        gateway=gateway,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="CapabilityRequest cannot be None",
    ):
        await service.execute(
            None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )

    gateway.execute.assert_not_awaited()

"""
Tests for LearningService.

Validates:

LearningService
        |
        v
CapabilityGateway
        |
        v
CapabilityRequest


This test verifies application orchestration only.

It does not test:

- Runtime executors;
- providers;
- persistence;
- LLM calls.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from backend.services.learning_service import (
    LearningService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityType,
)


class FakeCapabilityGateway:
    """
    Fake Runtime gateway.

    Captures the capability request submitted
    by the application service.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.received_request = None

    async def execute(
        self,
        request,
    ):
        self.received_request = request

        from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
            CapabilityResult,
        )

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=(
                CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
            ),
            output={
                "flashcards": 3,
            },
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_learning_service_creates_flashcard_request():
    """
    LearningService should create a Runtime
    FLASHCARD_GENERATION request.
    """

    gateway = FakeCapabilityGateway()

    service = LearningService(
        capability_gateway=gateway,
    )

    result = await service.generate_flashcards(
        request_id="learning-test-001",
        topic="French vocabulary",
        learner_context=(
            "B2 learner preparing exams"
        ),
    )

    assert result.status == (
        CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert gateway.received_request is not None

    assert gateway.received_request.capability == (
        CapabilityType.FLASHCARD_GENERATION
    )

    assert (
        gateway.received_request.payload["topic"]
        == "French vocabulary"
    )

    assert (
        gateway.received_request.payload[
            "learner_context"
        ]
        == "B2 learner preparing exams"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_learning_service_rejects_empty_topic():
    """
    Empty learning topics must fail validation.
    """

    gateway = FakeCapabilityGateway()

    service = LearningService(
        capability_gateway=gateway,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Topic cannot be empty",
    ):
        await service.generate_flashcards(
            request_id="learning-test-002",
            topic="",
        )


def test_learning_service_requires_gateway():
    """
    Gateway dependency is mandatory.
    """

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Capability gateway cannot be None",
    ):
        LearningService(
            capability_gateway=None,
        )

"""
Runtime API route integration tests.

Validates:

HTTP
 |
 v
FastAPI API Route
 |
 v
ExternalRuntimeService
 |
 v
CapabilityGateway
 |
 v
Runtime Response

The test uses controlled fakes.

It does not call:
- Parallel API
- Gemini
- Google Cloud
- external providers
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from backend.api.main import (
    create_app,
)

from backend.api import routes

from backend.services.external_runtime_service import (
    ExternalRuntimeService,
)


class FakeRuntimeGateway:
    """
    Controlled Runtime gateway for API testing.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ) -> dict[str, object]:
        return {
            "request_id": "test-runtime-request",
            "capability": "live_intelligence",
            "status": "completed",
            "output": {
                "results": [
                    {
                        "title": "AI News Result",
                        "source": "fake-provider",
                    }
                ]
            },
        }


def create_test_runtime_service() -> ExternalRuntimeService:
    """
    Create ExternalRuntimeService using
    a controlled gateway.
    """

    return ExternalRuntimeService(
        gateway=FakeRuntimeGateway(),
    )


def test_runtime_execute_api_route():
    """
    Validate:

    POST /runtime/execute

        ->
    ExternalRuntimeService

        ->
    Runtime Result
    """

    original_factory = (
        routes.create_external_runtime_service
    )

    routes.create_external_runtime_service = (
        create_test_runtime_service
    )

    try:
        app = create_app()

        client = TestClient(
            app,
        )

        response = client.post(
            "/runtime/execute",
            json={
                "capability": (
                    "live_intelligence"
                ),
                "payload": {
                    "query": (
                        "latest AI news"
                    ),
                },
                "user_id": (
                    "runtime-api-test-user"
                ),
            },
        )

    finally:
        routes.create_external_runtime_service = (
            original_factory
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    body = response.json()

    assert (
        body["status"]
        == "completed"
    )

    assert (
        body["capability"]
        == "live_intelligence"
    )

    assert (
        body["request_id"]
        == "test-runtime-request"
    )

    assert (
        body["output"]["results"][0]["source"]
        == "fake-provider"
    )


def test_runtime_execute_requires_capability():
    """
    Validate API transport validation.
    """

    app = create_app()

    client = TestClient(
        app,
    )

    response = client.post(
        "/runtime/execute",
        json={
            "payload": {},
        },
    )

    assert response.status_code == 422

"""
Application Factory Runtime tests.

Validates:

ApplicationFactory
        |
        v
RuntimeApplicationService
        |
        v
ExternalRuntimeService


The factory:

- creates application services;
- injects dependencies.

It does not:

- create providers;
- create agents;
- create gateways;
- execute capabilities.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


import pytest


from backend.application.factory import (
    ApplicationFactory,
)

from backend.services.external_runtime_service import (
    ExternalRuntimeService,
)

from backend.services.runtime.runtime_application_service import (
    RuntimeApplicationService,
)


class FakeGateway:
    """
    Controlled gateway.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        request,
    ):
        return {
            "request_id": "factory-test",
            "capability": request.capability,
            "status": "completed",
            "output": {},
        }


def test_factory_creates_external_runtime_service():
    """
    Factory should create ExternalRuntimeService
    from an existing gateway.
    """

    factory = ApplicationFactory()

    gateway = FakeGateway()

    service = (
        factory.create_external_runtime_service(
            gateway=gateway,
        )
    )

    assert isinstance(
        service,
        ExternalRuntimeService,
    )


def test_factory_creates_runtime_application_service():
    """
    Factory should wrap ExternalRuntimeService
    inside RuntimeApplicationService.
    """

    factory = ApplicationFactory()

    runtime_service = (
        ExternalRuntimeService(
            gateway=FakeGateway(),
        )
    )

    service = (
        factory.create_runtime_application_service(
            runtime_service=runtime_service,
        )
    )

    assert isinstance(
        service,
        RuntimeApplicationService,
    )


def test_factory_rejects_missing_runtime_service():
    """
    Factory should reject invalid dependencies.
    """

    factory = ApplicationFactory()

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):

        factory.create_runtime_application_service(
            runtime_service=None,
        )

"""
Runtime bootstrap flow tests.

Validates:

FastAPI startup
        |
        v
Application Bootstrap
        |
        v
External Runtime Service
        |
        v
Configured Runtime dependency

No external providers are called.
"""


from __future__ import annotations


import pytest

from backend.application.bootstrap import (
    bootstrap_application,
    get_external_runtime_service,
)



def test_runtime_bootstrap_creates_service():
    """
    Application bootstrap must create
    ExternalRuntimeService.
    """

    bootstrap_application()

    service = (
        get_external_runtime_service()
    )

    assert service is not None



def test_runtime_bootstrap_is_reusable():
    """
    Bootstrap should be deterministic.

    Calling bootstrap twice must not break
    the application boundary.
    """

    bootstrap_application()

    first = (
        get_external_runtime_service()
    )

    bootstrap_application()

    second = (
        get_external_runtime_service()
    )

    assert first is not None
    assert second is not None

"""
External Runtime Container tests.

Validates the application dependency boundary.

Flow:

bootstrap
    |
    v
ExternalRuntimeContainer
    |
    v
ExternalRuntimeService


The container:

- stores configured services;
- returns configured services.

It does not:

- compose Runtime;
- create providers;
- create gateways;
- execute capabilities.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


import pytest


from backend.runtime.external_runtime_container import (
    configure_external_runtime_service,
    get_external_runtime_service,
)

from backend.services.external_runtime_service import (
    ExternalRuntimeService,
)


class FakeGateway:
    """
    Controlled gateway.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        request,
    ):
        return {
            "status": "completed",
        }


@pytest.fixture
def runtime_service():
    """
    Create controlled ExternalRuntimeService.
    """

    return ExternalRuntimeService(
        gateway=FakeGateway(),
    )


def test_container_stores_runtime_service(
    runtime_service,
):
    """
    Container should store configured service.
    """

    configure_external_runtime_service(
        runtime_service,
    )

    configured = (
        get_external_runtime_service()
    )

    assert configured is runtime_service


def test_container_rejects_none():
    """
    Container must reject invalid configuration.
    """

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        configure_external_runtime_service(
            None,
        )

    ExternalRuntimeService,

    ExecutionContext,

class FakeRuntimeGateway:
    """
    Fake capability gateway.

    Simulates a successful Runtime execution
    without external dependencies.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ):
        return {
            "status": "completed",
            "capability": "live_intelligence",
            "results": [
                {
                    "title": "AI developments 2026",
                    "source": "fake_parallel",
                }
            ],
        }

    """
    Validate:

    Application
        ->
    External Runtime Service
        ->
    Capability Gateway
        ->
    Result
    """

    gateway = FakeRuntimeGateway()

    service = ExternalRuntimeService(
        gateway=gateway,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="runtime-test-user",
        request="Find latest AI developments.",
    )

    result = await service.execute(
        context,
    )

    assert result is not None

    assert result["status"] == "completed"

    assert (
        result["capability"]
        == "live_intelligence"
    )

    assert len(
        result["results"]
    ) == 1

    assert (
        result["results"][0]["source"]
        == "fake_parallel"

- external providers

    reset_bootstrap,

def reset_runtime_state():
    """
    Ensure bootstrap state is isolated between tests.
    """

    yield

def test_bootstrap_registers_external_runtime_service():
    """
    Bootstrap should create and register
    ExternalRuntimeService.

    service = (
        get_external_runtime_service()
    )

    assert service is not None

    """
    Calling bootstrap twice must not recreate
    application runtime unexpectedly.
    """

    bootstrap_application()

    first_service = (
        get_external_runtime_service()
    )

    bootstrap_application()

    second_service = (
        get_external_runtime_service()
    )

    assert first_service is second_service

"""
Tests for Challenge Generator.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceResponse,
)

from src.challenge_engine.generator import (
    ChallengeGenerator,
)

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    VocabularyItem,
)


class FakeContentGenerator:

    async def generate_vocabulary(
        self,
        summary,
        language,
        learner_level,
    ):
        return [
            VocabularyItem(
                term="innovation",
                definition="A new idea or approach.",
            )
        ]

    async def generate_questions(
        self,
        summary,
        language,
        learner_level,
    ):
        return [
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question="Quel est votre avis ?"
            )
        ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_generate_learning_challenge():

    generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeContentGenerator()
    )

    intelligence = LiveIntelligenceResponse(
        topic="AI Regulation",
        summary="AI regulation is evolving.",
        sources=[],
        key_information=[
            "governance"
        ],
    )

    result = await generator.generate(
        intelligence
    )

    assert result.topic == "AI Regulation"
    assert result.language == "French"
    assert result.vocabulary[0].term == "innovation"
    assert len(result.discussion_questions) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_generator_rejects_empty_summary():

    generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeContentGenerator()
    )

    intelligence = LiveIntelligenceResponse(
        topic="AI",
        summary="",
        sources=[],
        key_information=[],
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        await generator.generate(
            intelligence
        )

"""
Tests for Challenge Engine models.
"""

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    LearningChallenge,
    VocabularyItem,
)


def test_learning_challenge_creation():

    vocabulary = VocabularyItem(
        term="réglementation",
        definition="Rules and legal requirements.",
        example="La réglementation européenne évolue.",
    )

    question = DiscussionQuestion(
        question="Quel est votre avis sur cette réglementation ?"
    )

    challenge = LearningChallenge(
        title="AI Act Challenge",
        topic="Artificial Intelligence Regulation",
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
        summary="A challenge about AI regulation.",
        vocabulary=[vocabulary],
        discussion_questions=[question],
    )

    assert challenge.title == "AI Act Challenge"
    assert challenge.language == "French"
    assert challenge.vocabulary[0].term == "réglementation"
    assert challenge.discussion_questions[0].difficulty == "B2"


def test_models_are_immutable():

    challenge = LearningChallenge(
        title="Test",
        topic="Test topic",
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
        summary="Summary",
        vocabulary=[],
        discussion_questions=[],
    )

    try:
        challenge.title = "Changed"
        assert False
    except AttributeError:
        assert True

import pytest

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)


def test_conversation_request_is_created():

    request = ConversationRequest(
        system_instruction=(
            "You are a friendly learning companion."
        ),
        user_prompt=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence."
        ),
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
    )

    assert request.character_id == "yeti"

    assert request.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert request.user_prompt == (
        "Explain artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is False
    )

    assert request.context_length == 0


def test_conversation_request_accepts_intelligence_context():

    request = ConversationRequest(
        system_instruction=(
            "You are a friendly learning companion."
        ),
        user_prompt=(
            "Explain the latest AI regulation."
        ),
        conversation_context=(
            "LIVE INTELLIGENCE\n"
            "Research query: Latest AI regulation"
        ),
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is True
    )

    assert request.context_length > 0

    assert (
        "LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
        in request.conversation_context
    )


def test_conversation_request_rejects_empty_instruction():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="system_instruction cannot be empty",
    ):
        ConversationRequest(
            system_instruction=" ",
            user_prompt="Explain AI",
        )


def test_conversation_request_rejects_empty_prompt():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="user_prompt cannot be empty",
    ):
        ConversationRequest(
            system_instruction="You are helpful.",
            user_prompt=" ",
        )


def test_conversation_request_rejects_blank_context():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="conversation_context cannot be blank",
    ):
        ConversationRequest(
            system_instruction="You are helpful.",
            user_prompt="Explain AI",
            conversation_context=" ",
        )


def test_conversation_request_summary_is_safe():

    request = ConversationRequest(
        system_instruction="You are helpful.",
        user_prompt="Explain AI.",
        conversation_context="Private context.",
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
    )

    summary = request.summary()

    assert summary["character_id"] == "yeti"

    assert summary["character_name"] == "Yeti"

    assert summary["has_context"] == "True"

    assert "Private context." not in summary

    assert "Explain AI." not in summary

"""
Tests for APScheduler adapter boundary.

Uses fake scheduler to avoid infrastructure dependency.
"""

import pytest


from backend.cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_apscheduler_adapter import (
    CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter,
)


class FakeScheduler:

    def __init__(self):
        self.jobs = []
        self.running = False


    def add_job(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.jobs.append(kwargs)


    def start(self):
        self.running = True


    def shutdown(self):
        self.running = False



class FakeWorkflow:

    async def create_daily_report(self, data=None):
        return data


    async def create_weekly_report(self, data=None):
        return data



def test_apscheduler_registers_jobs():

    scheduler = FakeScheduler()

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=FakeWorkflow(),
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    assert len(scheduler.jobs) == 2



@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_with_scheduler_adapter():

    scheduler = FakeScheduler()

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=FakeWorkflow(),
    )

    from backend.cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime import (
        CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime,
    )

    runtime = CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime(
        adapter
    )

    await runtime.start()

    assert runtime.status()["started"] is True

    await runtime.stop()

    assert runtime.status()["started"] is False

        CybersecurityNotificationPolicy()
    )

        policy.should_send_daily_report()
        is True
    )


    assert (
        policy.should_send_weekly_report()
        is True
    )

        policy.should_send_critical_alert(
            {
                "critical_count": 1
            }
        )
        is True
    )

        CybersecurityNotificationPolicy(
            critical_alerts_enabled=False

    )


    assert (
        policy.should_send_critical_alert(
            {
                "critical_count": 1
            }
        )
        is False
    )

    CybersecurityReportScheduler,

class FakeReportService:

    async def create_daily_brief(self, intelligence):
        return {"type": "daily"}

    async def create_weekly_brief(self, intelligence):
        return {"type": "weekly"}

    async def process_security_event(self, event):
        return {"type": "critical"}


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_scheduler_daily_weekly_critical():

    scheduler = CybersecurityReportScheduler(
        FakeReportService()
    )

    assert await scheduler.trigger_daily_report({}) == {
        "type": "daily"
    }

    assert await scheduler.trigger_weekly_report({}) == {
        "type": "weekly"
    }

    result = await scheduler.process_critical_event({})

    assert result["type"] == "critical"

===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\cybersecurity\test_delivery_control.py
===============================================================================
"""
Cybersecurity Delivery Control tests.

Validates the human governance kill switch for
cybersecurity notification delivery.

Tests:
- initial active state;
- pause;
- paused status;
- resume;
- restored active state.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


from backend.cybersecurity.control.cybersecurity_delivery_control import (
    CybersecurityDeliveryControl,
)


def test_delivery_control_starts_active() -> None:
    control = CybersecurityDeliveryControl()

    assert control.is_paused() is False

    status = control.status()

    assert status["paused"] is False
    assert status["reason"] is None


def test_delivery_control_pause_blocks_delivery() -> None:
    control = CybersecurityDeliveryControl()

    control.pause(
        reason="Security incident investigation",
    )

    assert control.is_paused() is True

    status = control.status()

    assert status["paused"] is True
    assert (
        status["reason"]
        == "Security incident investigation"
    )
    assert status["changed_at"] is not None


def test_delivery_control_resume_restores_delivery() -> None:
    control = CybersecurityDeliveryControl()

    control.pause(
        reason="Emergency stop",
    )

    assert control.is_paused() is True

    control.resume()

    assert control.is_paused() is False

    status = control.status()

    assert status["paused"] is False
    assert status["reason"] is None
    assert status["changed_at"] is not None


def test_delivery_control_can_be_paused_and_resumed_multiple_times() -> None:
    control = CybersecurityDeliveryControl()

    control.pause(
        reason="First emergency stop",
    )

    assert control.is_paused() is True

    control.resume()

    assert control.is_paused() is False

    control.pause(
        reason="Second emergency stop",
    )

    assert control.is_paused() is True

    status = control.status()

    assert status["reason"] == "Second emergency stop"

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\cybersecurity\test_real_scheduler_adapter.py
===============================================================================
"""
APScheduler Adapter tests.

Validates:

- jobs register;
- lifecycle works;
- configuration is applied.

Does NOT:
- send emails;
- execute reports.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


from backend.cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_apscheduler_adapter import (
    CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter,
)



class FakeScheduler:

    def __init__(self):

        self.jobs = []

        self.running = False



    def add_job(
        self,
        function,
        **kwargs,
    ):

        self.jobs.append(
            kwargs
        )



    def start(
        self,
    ):

        self.running = True



    def shutdown(
        self,
    ):

        self.running = False



class FakeWorkflow:


    async def create_daily_report(
        self,
    ):
        pass



    async def create_weekly_report(
        self,
    ):
        pass




def test_apscheduler_adapter_registers_jobs():


    scheduler = (
        FakeScheduler()
    )


    adapter = (
        CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
            scheduler=scheduler,
            workflow_service=FakeWorkflow(),
        )
    )


    adapter.register_jobs()


    assert len(
        scheduler.jobs
    ) == 2



def test_apscheduler_adapter_lifecycle():


    scheduler = (
        FakeScheduler()
    )


    adapter = (
        CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
            scheduler=scheduler,
            workflow_service=FakeWorkflow(),
        )
    )


    adapter.start()


    assert (
        scheduler.running
        is True
    )


    adapter.shutdown()


    assert (
        scheduler.running
        is False
    )

        self,

    ):
        return event

    async def create_daily_email(
        self,
        report,
    ):
        return {
            "type": "daily_email"
        }



    async def create_weekly_email(
        self,
        report,
    ):
        return {
            "type": "weekly_email"
        }



    async def create_critical_alert(
        self,
        report,
    ):
        return {
            "type": "critical_email"
        }

"""
Tests for Cybersecurity Scheduler Runtime.

Validates:
- scheduler starts;
- jobs register;
- scheduler stops.

Does NOT test:
- FastAPI;
- routers;
- APIs;
- email providers.
"""


import pytest


from backend.cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime import (
    CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime,
)


from backend.cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_job_registry import (
    CybersecurityJobRegistry,
)



@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_scheduler_runtime_start_registers_jobs():

    registry = (
        CybersecurityJobRegistry()
    )


    runtime = (
        CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime(
            job_registry=registry,
        )
    )


    await runtime.start()


    status = (
        runtime.status()
    )


    assert (
        status["started"]
        is True
    )


    assert (
        len(status["jobs"])
        == 3
    )



@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_scheduler_runtime_shutdown():

    registry = (
        CybersecurityJobRegistry()
    )


    runtime = (
        CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime(
            job_registry=registry,
        )
    )


    await runtime.start()


    await runtime.stop()


    status = (
        runtime.status()
    )


    assert (
        status["started"]
        is False
    )

"""

from src.evaluation.criteria import (
    COMPLETENESS,
    DEFAULT_CRITERIA,
    EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
    FACTUALITY,
    LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
    SOURCE_COVERAGE,
)


def test_default_criteria_contains_expected_criteria():

    names = {
        criterion.name
        for criterion in DEFAULT_CRITERIA
    }

    assert names == {
        "factuality",
        "completeness",
        "language_level",
        "educational_value",
        "source_coverage",
    }


def test_factuality_has_highest_weight():

    assert FACTUALITY.weight == 2.0


def test_educational_value_weight():

    assert EDUCATIONAL_VALUE.weight == 1.5


def test_source_coverage_weight():

    assert SOURCE_COVERAGE.weight == 1.5


def test_all_criteria_have_valid_weights():

    for criterion in DEFAULT_CRITERIA:
        assert criterion.weight > 0


def test_all_criteria_have_descriptions():

    for criterion in DEFAULT_CRITERIA:
        assert criterion.description.strip()


def test_language_level_criterion():

    assert LANGUAGE_LEVEL.name == "language_level"


def test_completeness_criterion():

    assert COMPLETENESS.name == "completeness"

"""
Tests for DeterministicEvaluator.
"""

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    LearningChallenge,
    VocabularyItem,
)

from src.evaluation.deterministic_evaluator import (
    DeterministicEvaluator,
)


def _create_valid_challenge() -> LearningChallenge:
    return LearningChallenge(
        title="AI Act Learning Challenge",
        topic="AI Act",
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
        summary=(
            "The European Union AI Act establishes "
            "a risk-based framework."
        ),
        vocabulary=[
            VocabularyItem(
                term="gouvernance",
                definition=(
                    "Organisation et contrôle "
                    "d'une activité."
                ),
            )
        ],
        discussion_questions=[
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la gouvernance de "
                    "l'IA est-elle importante ?"
                )
            )
        ],
    )


def test_evaluator_returns_expected_criteria():

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        _create_valid_challenge()
    )

    names = {
        score.criterion
        for score in scores
    }

    assert names == {
        "completeness",
        "language_level",
        "educational_value",
        "source_coverage",
    }


def test_valid_challenge_scores_completeness():

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        _create_valid_challenge()
    )

    score = next(
        score
        for score in scores
        if score.criterion == "completeness"
    )

    assert score.score == 1.0


def test_valid_challenge_scores_language_level():

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        _create_valid_challenge()
    )

    score = next(
        score
        for score in scores
        if score.criterion == "language_level"
    )

    assert score.score == 1.0


def test_valid_challenge_scores_educational_value():

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        _create_valid_challenge()
    )

    score = next(
        score
        for score in scores
        if score.criterion == "educational_value"
    )

    assert score.score == 1.0


def test_empty_vocabulary_reduces_educational_score():

    challenge = _create_valid_challenge()

    challenge = LearningChallenge(
        title=challenge.title,
        topic=challenge.topic,
        language=challenge.language,
        learner_level=challenge.learner_level,
        summary=challenge.summary,
        vocabulary=[],
        discussion_questions=challenge.discussion_questions,
    )

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(challenge)

    score = next(
        score
        for score in scores
        if score.criterion == "educational_value"
    )

    assert score.score == 0.5


def test_invalid_language_level_scores_zero():

    challenge = _create_valid_challenge()

    challenge = LearningChallenge(
        title=challenge.title,
        topic=challenge.topic,
        language=challenge.language,
        learner_level="INVALID",
        summary=challenge.summary,
        vocabulary=challenge.vocabulary,
        discussion_questions=challenge.discussion_questions,
    )

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(challenge)

    score = next(
        score
        for score in scores
        if score.criterion == "language_level"
    )

    assert score.score == 0.0


def test_missing_summary_reduces_completeness():

    challenge = _create_valid_challenge()

    challenge = LearningChallenge(
        title=challenge.title,
        topic=challenge.topic,
        language=challenge.language,
        learner_level=challenge.learner_level,
        summary="",
        vocabulary=challenge.vocabulary,
        discussion_questions=challenge.discussion_questions,
    )

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(challenge)

    score = next(
        score
        for score in scores
        if score.criterion == "completeness"
    )

    assert score.score < 1.0

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\evaluation\test_engine.py
===============================================================================
"""
Tests for AI Evaluation Engine.
"""

import pytest

from src.evaluation.engine import EvaluationEngine
from src.evaluation.models import CriterionScore


CRITERIA = [
    {
        "name": "factuality",
        "description": "Factual accuracy.",
        "weight": 2.0,
    },
    {
        "name": "completeness",
        "description": "Completeness.",
        "weight": 1.0,
    },
]


def _create_engine() -> EvaluationEngine:
    from src.evaluation.models import EvaluationCriterion

    return EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            EvaluationCriterion(**criterion)
            for criterion in CRITERIA
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )


def test_engine_calculates_weighted_score():

    engine = _create_engine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        [
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="factuality",
                score=1.0,
                reasoning="Fully supported.",
            ),
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="completeness",
                score=0.5,
                reasoning="Partially complete.",
            ),
        ]
    )

    # (1.0 * 2 + 0.5 * 1) / 3
    assert result.overall_score == pytest.approx(
        0.8333333333
    )

    assert result.passed is True


def test_engine_can_fail_evaluation():

    engine = _create_engine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        [
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="factuality",
                score=0.5,
                reasoning="Partially supported.",
            ),
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="completeness",
                score=0.4,
                reasoning="Incomplete.",
            ),
        ]
    )

    assert result.overall_score == pytest.approx(
        0.4666666667
    )

    assert result.passed is False


def test_engine_rejects_empty_criteria():

    from src.evaluation.engine import EvaluationEngine

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        EvaluationEngine(criteria=[])


def test_engine_rejects_invalid_passing_score():

    from src.evaluation.models import EvaluationCriterion

    criterion = EvaluationCriterion(
        name="factuality",
        description="Factual accuracy.",
        weight=1.0,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        EvaluationEngine(
            criteria=[criterion],
            passing_score=1.5,
        )


def test_engine_rejects_unknown_criterion():

    engine = _create_engine()

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        engine.evaluate(
            [
                CriterionScore(
                    criterion="unknown",
                    score=0.9,
                    reasoning="Invalid.",
                )
            ]
        )


def test_engine_rejects_invalid_score():

    engine = _create_engine()

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        engine.evaluate(
            [
                CriterionScore(
                    criterion="factuality",
                    score=1.5,
                    reasoning="Invalid score.",
                )
            ]
        )


def test_engine_rejects_duplicate_criteria():

    engine = _create_engine()

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        engine.evaluate(
            [
                CriterionScore(
                    criterion="factuality",
                    score=0.9,
                    reasoning="First score.",
                ),
                CriterionScore(
                    criterion="factuality",
                    score=0.8,
                    reasoning="Duplicate score.",
                ),
            ]
        )
