===============================================================================
"""
Tests for AI Evaluation domain models.
"""

from src.evaluation.models import (
    CriterionScore,
    EvaluationCriterion,
    EvaluationResult,
)


def test_evaluation_criterion_creation():

    criterion = EvaluationCriterion(
        name="factuality",
        description="Information is factually supported.",
        weight=2.0,
    )

    assert criterion.name == "factuality"
    assert criterion.weight == 2.0


def test_criterion_score_creation():

    score = CriterionScore(
        criterion="factuality",
        score=0.9,
        reasoning="Claims are supported by sources.",
    )

    assert score.criterion == "factuality"
    assert score.score == 0.9


def test_evaluation_result_creation():

    result = EvaluationResult(
        overall_score=0.85,
        scores=[
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="factuality",
                score=0.9,
                reasoning="Supported.",
            )
        ],
        passed=True,
    )

    assert result.overall_score == 0.85
    assert result.passed is True
    assert result.evaluator == "deterministic"


def test_evaluation_result_is_immutable():

    result = EvaluationResult(
        overall_score=0.8,
        scores=[],
        passed=True,
    )

    try:
        result.overall_score = 0.5
        assert False
    except AttributeError:
        assert True

"""
Tests for AI Evaluation Quality Report.
"""

from src.evaluation.models import (
    CriterionScore,
    EvaluationResult,
)

from src.evaluation.report import (
    EvaluationReport,
)


def _create_result() -> EvaluationResult:
    return EvaluationResult(
        overall_score=0.85,
        scores=[
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="completeness",
                score=1.0,
                reasoning="All required components are present.",
            ),
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="language_level",
                score=0.7,
                reasoning="CEFR level is valid.",
            ),
        ],
        passed=True,
    )


def test_report_contains_overall_score():

    report = EvaluationReport()

    output = report.generate(
        _create_result()
    )

    assert "Overall Score: 0.85" in output


def test_report_contains_pass_status():

    report = EvaluationReport()

    output = report.generate(
        _create_result()
    )

    assert "Status: PASS" in output


def test_report_contains_evaluator():

    report = EvaluationReport()

    output = report.generate(
        _create_result()
    )

    assert "Evaluator: deterministic" in output


def test_report_contains_criteria():

    report = EvaluationReport()

    output = report.generate(
        _create_result()
    )

    assert "completeness: 1.00" in output
    assert "language_level: 0.70" in output


def test_report_contains_reasoning():

    report = EvaluationReport()

    output = report.generate(
        _create_result()
    )

    assert (
        "All required components are present."
        in output
    )


def test_failed_report_shows_fail():

    report = EvaluationReport()

    result = EvaluationResult(
        overall_score=0.45,
        scores=[
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="completeness",
                score=0.45,
                reasoning="Several components are missing.",
            )
        ],
        passed=False,
    )

    output = report.generate(result)

    assert "Overall Score: 0.45" in output
    assert "Status: FAIL" in output

from backend.governance.change_management.change_audit_bridge import (
    ChangeAuditBridge,
)


class FakeAudit:

    def __init__(self):
        self.events = []

    def record(self, **kwargs):
        self.events.append(kwargs)
        return kwargs


def test_change_audit_bridge():

    audit = FakeAudit()
    bridge = ChangeAuditBridge(audit)

    class FakeChange:
        change_id = "CHG-001"
        component = "provider"
        reason = "incident"

        class change_type:
            value = "PROVIDER"

        class risk:
            value = "CRITICAL"

        class status:
            value = "APPROVED"

    result = bridge.record_change_event(
        "CHANGE_APPROVED",
        FakeChange(),
        "admin",
    )

    assert result["event"] == "CHANGE_APPROVED"
    assert len(audit.events) == 1

===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\governance\test_change_management_core.py
===============================================================================
from backend.governance.change_management.change_management_service import (
    ChangeManagementService,
)
from backend.governance.change_management.change_risk import ChangeRisk
from backend.governance.change_management.change_status import ChangeStatus
from backend.governance.change_management.change_type import ChangeType


def test_provider_change_is_created_pending_approval():

    service = ChangeManagementService()

    record = service.create_change(
        component="openai",
        action="PAUSE_PROVIDER",
        reason="Provider incident",
        requested_by="admin",
        change_type=ChangeType.PROVIDER,
        risk=ChangeRisk.CRITICAL,
        approval_id="APR-001",
    )

    assert record.change_id == "CHG-001"
    assert record.change_type == ChangeType.PROVIDER
    assert record.risk == ChangeRisk.CRITICAL
    assert record.status == ChangeStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL


def test_model_change_is_supported():

    service = ChangeManagementService()

    record = service.create_change(
        component="gpt-5-mini",
        action="CHANGE_MODEL_CONFIGURATION",
        reason="Performance improvement",
        requested_by="admin",
        change_type=ChangeType.MODEL,
        risk=ChangeRisk.HIGH,
    )

    assert record.change_type == ChangeType.MODEL
    assert record.status == ChangeStatus.PROPOSED


def test_change_lifecycle_is_explicit():

    service = ChangeManagementService()

    record = service.create_change(
        component="scheduler",
        action="CHANGE_SCHEDULE",
        reason="Maintenance window",
        requested_by="admin",
        change_type=ChangeType.SCHEDULER,
        risk=ChangeRisk.MEDIUM,
    )

    record = service.mark_approved(record.change_id)
    assert record.status == ChangeStatus.APPROVED

    record = service.mark_scheduled(record.change_id)
    assert record.status == ChangeStatus.SCHEDULED

    record = service.mark_executed(record.change_id)
    assert record.status == ChangeStatus.EXECUTED


def test_change_management_does_not_execute_controls():

    service = ChangeManagementService()

    record = service.create_change(
        component="email_provider",
        action="PAUSE_PROVIDER",
        reason="Incident",
        requested_by="admin",
        change_type=ChangeType.PROVIDER,
        risk=ChangeRisk.CRITICAL,
    )

    assert record.status == ChangeStatus.PROPOSED
    assert not hasattr(service, "execute")
    assert not hasattr(service, "pause_provider")

"""
Tests for AI Governance Policy Engine.
"""

from src.governance.engine import (
    GovernancePolicyEngine,
)

from src.governance.models import (
    GovernanceDecision,
    GovernancePolicy,
    RiskLevel,
)


def test_low_risk_capability_is_allowed():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="content_formatting",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.LOW,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW
    assert result.risk_level == RiskLevel.LOW


def test_external_research_can_be_allowed():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="live_web_research",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
            allows_external_calls=True,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW

    assert any(
        "External calls" in reason
        for reason in result.reasons
    )


def test_human_review_policy_requires_review():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="high_impact_decision",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
            requires_human_review=True,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.REVIEW


def test_high_risk_capability_requires_review():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="high_risk_operation",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.HIGH,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.REVIEW


def test_critical_risk_is_blocked():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="critical_operation",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.CRITICAL,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.BLOCK


def test_side_effects_are_blocked():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="external_modification",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
            allows_side_effects=True,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.BLOCK

    assert (
        "Uncontrolled side effects are prohibited."
        in result.reasons
    )


def test_side_effect_block_takes_precedence_over_review():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="dangerous_operation",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.HIGH,
            requires_human_review=True,
            allows_side_effects=True,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.BLOCK


def test_critical_risk_takes_precedence_over_review():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="critical_operation",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.CRITICAL,
            requires_human_review=True,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.BLOCK

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\governance\test_governance_audit_api.py
===============================================================================
import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from httpx import ASGITransport, AsyncClient

from backend.governance.api.governance_audit_api import (
    get_governance_audit_service,
    router,
)
from backend.governance.governance_audit_service import (
    GovernanceAuditService,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_governance_audit_api_returns_recorded_events():

    audit_service = GovernanceAuditService()

    audit_service.record(
        event="PROVIDER_PAUSED",
        actor="admin",
        reason="Provider incident",
        component="provider_management",
    )

    application = FastAPI()
    application.include_router(router)

    application.dependency_overrides[
        get_governance_audit_service
    ] = lambda: audit_service

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=application,
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.get(
            "/governance/audit/events"
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    payload = response.json()

    assert payload["count"] == 1
    assert len(payload["events"]) == 1

    event = payload["events"][0]

    assert event["event"] == "PROVIDER_PAUSED"
    assert event["actor"] == "admin"
    assert event["reason"] == "Provider incident"
    assert event["component"] == "provider_management"
    assert event["timestamp"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_governance_audit_api_returns_empty_store():

        get_governance_audit_service

        event="CYBERSECURITY_DELIVERY_PAUSED",
        actor="admin",
        reason="Security incident",
        component="cybersecurity_delivery",
    )

    application = FastAPI()
    application.include_router(router)

    application.dependency_overrides[
        get_governance_audit_service
    ] = lambda: audit_service

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=application,
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.get(
            "/governance/audit/events"
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert audit_service.list_events()[0].event == (
        "CYBERSECURITY_DELIVERY_PAUSED"
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=ASGITransport(app=app),
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.post(
            "/governance/changes",
            json={
                "component": "openai",
                "action": "PAUSE_PROVIDER",
                "reason": "Provider incident",
                "requested_by": "admin",
                "change_type": "PROVIDER",
                "risk": "CRITICAL",
                "approval_id": "APR-001",
            },
        )

    body = response.json()

    assert body["change_id"].startswith("CHG-")
    assert body["change_type"] == "PROVIDER"
    assert body["risk"] == "CRITICAL"
    assert body["status"] == "PENDING_APPROVAL"

"""
Tests for AI Governance domain models.
"""

from src.governance.models import (
    GovernanceDecision,
    GovernancePolicy,
    GovernanceResult,
    RiskLevel,
)


def test_governance_policy_creation():

    policy = GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_web_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )

    assert policy.capability == "live_web_research"
    assert policy.risk_level == RiskLevel.MEDIUM
    assert policy.allows_external_calls is True
    assert policy.allows_side_effects is False


def test_governance_result_creation():

    result = GovernanceResult(
        capability="live_web_research",
        decision=GovernanceDecision.ALLOW,
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        reasons=("External research is permitted.",),
    )

    assert result.capability == "live_web_research"
    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW
    assert result.risk_level == RiskLevel.MEDIUM
    assert len(result.reasons) == 1


def test_risk_levels_are_defined():

    assert RiskLevel.LOW.value == "low"
    assert RiskLevel.MEDIUM.value == "medium"
    assert RiskLevel.HIGH.value == "high"
    assert RiskLevel.CRITICAL.value == "critical"


def test_governance_decisions_are_defined():

    assert GovernanceDecision.ALLOW.value == "allow"
    assert GovernanceDecision.REVIEW.value == "review"
    assert GovernanceDecision.BLOCK.value == "block"


def test_governance_policy_is_immutable():

    policy = GovernancePolicy(
        capability="test",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.LOW,
    )

    try:
        policy.capability = "changed"
        assert False
    except AttributeError:
        assert True


def test_governance_result_is_immutable():

    result = GovernanceResult(
        capability="test",
        decision=GovernanceDecision.ALLOW,
        risk_level=RiskLevel.LOW,
        reasons=(),
    )

    try:
        result.decision = GovernanceDecision.BLOCK
        assert False
    except AttributeError:
        assert True

import pytest

from fastapi import FastAPI

from httpx import (
    ASGITransport,
    AsyncClient,
)

from backend.governance.api.governance_operations_api import (
    router,
    get_dashboard_service,
)

    GovernanceDashboardService,
)


class FakeControl:

    def __init__(self):
        self.paused = True

    def is_paused(self):
        return self.paused

    def status(self):
        return {
            "paused": True,
            "reason": "Security incident",
            "changed_at": "2026-08-21T10:00:00Z",
        }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_operations_center_returns_governance_state():

    service = GovernanceDashboardService(
        provider_control=FakeControl()
    )

    app = FastAPI()

    app.include_router(router)

    app.dependency_overrides[
        get_dashboard_service
    ] = lambda: service


    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.get(
            "/operations/governance/status"
        )


    assert response.status_code == 200

    body = response.json()

    assert (
        body["controls"][0]["status"]
        == "paused"
    )

    assert (
        "Security incident"
        in body["yeti"]["message"]
    )

===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_api_chat_flow.py
===============================================================================
"""
API chat flow integration tests.

Verifies the complete API boundary for the /chat endpoint
using a deterministic CompanionService test double.

Architecture:

HTTP
 |
 v
FastAPI /chat
 |
 v
CompanionService
 |
 v
ConversationAgent
 |
 v
LLMService
 |
 v
LLMProvider

External providers are intentionally not called.
"""

import pytest

from httpx import (
    ASGITransport,
    AsyncClient,
)

from backend.api import routes
from backend.api.main import app


class FakeCompanionService:
    """
    Deterministic CompanionService for API integration tests.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        character,
    ) -> None:
        self.character = character
        self.context = None
        self.use_research = None

    async def interact(
        self,
        context,
        use_research=False,
    ):
        """
        Capture the API-to-service contract and return
        a deterministic companion result.
        """

        self.context = context
        self.use_research = use_research

        return {
            "character": self.character.name,
            "conversation": {
                "character": self.character.name,
                "message": "Integration test response",
            },
            "research": (
                {
                    "query": context.request,
                }
                if use_research
                else None
            ),
            "quality": {
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
                "findings": [],
                "recommendations": [],
            },
            "memory_count": 0,
        }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_chat_endpoint_executes_companion_service(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Verify that /chat creates the correct execution
    context and delegates to CompanionService.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory(
        character,
    ):
        service = FakeCompanionService(
            character
        )

        captured["service"] = service

        return service

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "create_companion_service",
        fake_factory,
    )

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.post(
            "/chat",
            json={
                "user_id": "integration-user",
                "character": "yeti",
                "message": (
                    "Explain artificial intelligence"
                ),
                "research": False,
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    service = captured["service"]

    assert service.context is not None

    assert (
        service.context.user_id
        == "integration-user"
    )

    assert (
        service.context.request
        == "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert service.use_research is False

    data = response.json()

    assert data["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert (
        data["conversation"]["message"]
        == "Integration test response"
    )

    assert data["research"] is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_chat_endpoint_enables_research(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Verify that the API correctly forwards the
    research flag to CompanionService.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory(
        character,
    ):
        service = FakeCompanionService(
            character
        )

        captured["service"] = service

        return service

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "create_companion_service",
        fake_factory,
    )

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.post(
            "/chat",
            json={
                "user_id": "integration-user",
                "character": "yeti",
                "message": (
                    "What is the EU AI Act?"
                ),
                "research": True,
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    service = captured["service"]

    assert service.use_research is True

    assert service.context is not None

    assert (
        service.context.request
        == "What is the EU AI Act?"
    )

    data = response.json()

    assert data["research"] is not None

    assert (
        data["research"]["query"]
        == "What is the EU AI Act?"
    )


===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_api_provider_wiring.py
===============================================================================
"""
API provider wiring integration tests.

Verifies that the API composition layer creates an
LLMService through the LLMProviderFactory and injects
that service into ConversationAgent.

The real OpenAI API is never called.
"""

import pytest

from backend.api import routes
from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)
from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)
from backend.services.llm_service import (
    LLMService,
)


class FakeProvider:
    """
    Deterministic provider used by the integration test.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.received_request = None

    async def generate(
        self,
        request,
    ):
        self.received_request = request

        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output="Fake AI response",
        )


class FakeConversationAgent:
    """
    Test double for ConversationAgent.

    Captures the injected LLMService so the API
    composition boundary can be verified.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        character,
        llm_service=None,
    ):
        self.character = character
        self.llm_service = llm_service


def create_character():
    """
    Create deterministic test character.
    """

    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


def test_create_companion_service_injects_llm_service(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Verify API composition creates an LLMService
    through the provider factory and injects it
    into ConversationAgent.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory_create(
        provider="openai",
    ):
        fake_provider = FakeProvider()

        captured["provider"] = fake_provider
        captured["provider_name"] = provider

        return fake_provider

    def fake_conversation_agent(
        character,
        llm_service=None,
    ):
        agent = FakeConversationAgent(
            character=character,
            llm_service=llm_service,
        )

        captured["character"] = character
        captured["agent"] = agent
        captured["llm_service"] = llm_service

        return agent

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes.LLMProviderFactory,
        "create",
        staticmethod(
            fake_factory_create
        ),
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "ConversationAgent",
        fake_conversation_agent,
    )

    character = (
        routes.character_registry.get(
            "yeti"
        )
    )

    service = (
        routes.create_companion_service(
            character
        )
    )

    assert service is not None

    assert (
        captured["character"]
        == character
    )

    assert (
        captured["llm_service"]
        is not None
    )

    assert isinstance(
        captured["llm_service"],
        LLMService,
    )

    assert (
        captured["provider"]
        is captured[
            "llm_service"
        ].llm_provider
    )

    assert (
        captured["provider_name"]
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        captured["agent"].llm_service
        is captured["llm_service"]
    )


===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_conversation_agent_provider_integration.py
===============================================================================
"""
API provider wiring integration test.

Verifies that the API composition layer injects
the LLM provider into ConversationAgent.

The OpenAI API call is replaced with a fake provider.
"""

import pytest

from backend.api import routes
from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class FakeLLMProvider:
    """
    Fake provider replacing OpenAI.

    Ensures API composition injects an LLM provider
    without calling external services.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.received_request = None

    async def generate(
        self,
        request,
    ):
        self.received_request = request

        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output=(
                "Fake AI response"
            ),
        )


def create_character():

    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_receives_provider():

    provider = FakeLLMProvider()

    from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
        ConversationAgent,
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character(),
        llm_provider=provider,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="integration-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert (
        result.output["message"]
        == "Fake AI response"
    )

    assert (
        result.output["provider_id"]
        == "fake_llm"
    )

    assert (
        provider.received_request.user_prompt
        == "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )


===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_discovery_notification_flow.py

"""
Integration test for the character-driven discovery notification.

Flow:

Memory
    ↓
DiscoveryService
    ↓
FlashDealCard
    ↓
DiscoveryNotification
    ↓
CommerceAction
    ↓
CommerceService
    ↓
Vendor handoff
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionStatus,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_deal,
    build_demo_memory,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.notification import (
    DiscoveryNotification,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.service import (
    CharacterContext,
    DiscoveryRequest,
    DiscoveryService,
)


def build_flash_deal():
    """
    Build the same application-level FlashDealCard used
    by the hackathon discovery flow.
    """

    deal = build_demo_deal()
    memory = build_demo_memory()

    request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        deal=deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    return memory, card


def test_notification_is_created_from_real_discovery_result():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-001",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    assert notification.user_id == memory.user_id

    assert notification.character_id == (
        card.character_id
    )

    assert notification.character_name == (
        card.character_name
    )

    assert notification.deal is card

    assert notification.product_name == (
        card.product_name
    )


def test_notification_preserves_personalisation():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-002",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    assert notification.relevance_reason == (
        card.relevance_reason
    )

    assert notification.relevance_reason


def test_notification_render_is_character_driven():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-003",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    rendered = notification.render()

    assert "Yeti:" in rendered

    assert (
        "Look what I found!"
        in rendered
    )

    assert card.product_name in rendered

    assert (
        card.relevance_reason
        in rendered
    )


def test_notification_does_not_change_commerce_flow():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-004",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id=notification.user_id,
        card=notification.deal,
    )

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert result.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )


def test_notification_does_not_create_a_shop():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-005",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    # The notification exposes one discovery event,
    # not a catalogue, cart, or shopping dashboard.

    assert notification.deal is card

    assert not hasattr(
        notification,
        "products",
    )

    assert not hasattr(
        notification,
        "cart",
    )

    assert not hasattr(
        notification,
        "checkout",
    )


===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_flash_deal_commerce_flow.py
===============================================================================
"""
Integration test for the complete Flash Deal commerce flow.

Flow:

Memory / personalisation
        ↓
DealCard
        ↓
FlashDealCard
        ↓
CommerceAction
        ↓
CommerceService
        ↓
External vendor handoff

No payment is executed by the test.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionStatus,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_flash_deal_card,
)


def test_complete_flash_deal_to_commerce_flow():
    """
    Validate the complete hackathon commerce boundary.
    """

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    commerce = FlashDealCommerce()

    action = commerce.create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=card,
    )

    assert action.user_id == "demo-user"

    assert action.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        action.action_type
        == CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert result.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    assert result.action_id == action.action_id


def test_commerce_flow_does_not_change_discovery_data():
    """
    Commerce must not mutate the discovered Flash Deal.
    """

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    original_product = card.product_name
    original_price = card.current_price
    original_vendor = card.vendor_name
    original_deal_id = card.deal_id

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=card,
    )

    CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert card.product_name == original_product
    assert card.current_price == original_price
    assert card.vendor_name == original_vendor
    assert card.deal_id == original_deal_id

===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_hackathon_complete_vertical_slice.py
===============================================================================
"""
End-to-end hackathon vertical-slice test.

Validates the working EasyChatBot journey:

Governance
    ↓
Live Intelligence
    ↓
Learning Challenge
    ↓
Quality Evaluation
    ↓
Memory
    ↓
DiscoveryService
    ↓
FlashDealCard
    ↓
CommerceAction
    ↓
CommerceService
    ↓
Vendor handoff

No real purchase or external payment is executed.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionStatus,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_deal,
    build_demo_memory,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.service import (
    CharacterContext,
    DiscoveryRequest,
    DiscoveryService,
)


def test_complete_hackathon_vertical_slice():

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # MEMORY
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    memory = build_demo_memory()

    assert memory.user_id == "demo-user"

    assert memory.content

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # DISCOVERY INPUT
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    deal = build_demo_deal()

    request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        deal=deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # DISCOVERY SERVICE
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert card.deal_id == deal.deal_id

    assert card.character_id == "yeti"

    assert card.product_name == deal.product_name

    assert card.vendor_name == deal.vendor

    assert card.relevance_reason

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # COMMERCE ACTION
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        card=card,
    )

    assert action.user_id == memory.user_id

    assert action.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        action.action_type
        == CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )

    assert (
        action.destination_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # COMMERCE SERVICE
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert result.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert result.action_id == action.action_id

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # SAFETY / SCOPE BOUNDARY
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        result.message
        == (
            "Continue to the approved vendor "
            "to complete the purchase."
        )
    )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_hackathon_demo_final.py

"""
Final end-to-end acceptance test for the EasyChatBot hackathon demo.

This test executes the real hackathon demonstration and verifies
the complete product story:

Governance
    ↓
Live Intelligence
    ↓
Learning
    ↓
Evaluation
    ↓
Personalised Discovery
    ↓
Character Notification
    ↓
Commerce Boundary
    ↓
Vendor Handoff

No real external purchase is performed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_demo.py"
)


def run_demo() -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    """Execute the real hackathon demo."""

    return subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )


def test_complete_hackathon_demo_succeeds():

    result = run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Hackathon demonstration failed.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )

    output = result.stdout

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Governance
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "STEP 1 — GOVERNANCE" in output
    assert "Decision: ALLOW" in output
    assert "Governance check: PASSED" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Live Intelligence
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "STEP 2 — LIVE INTELLIGENCE" in output
    assert "Topic: AI Act" in output
    assert "Language: French" in output
    assert "Learner level: B2" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Learning
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "Summary:" in output
    assert "Vocabulary:" in output
    assert "Discussion questions:" in output

    assert "gouvernance" in output
    assert "réglementation" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Evaluation
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "STEP 3 — AI QUALITY EVALUATION" in output
    assert "Status: PASS" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Personalised Discovery
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "STEP 4 — PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
        in output
    )

    assert "## Yeti" in output
    assert "### NEW FIND!" in output
    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output
    assert "Pro. Beautiful. Powerful." in output

    assert "### HIGHLIGHTS" in output
    assert "Pro camera system" in output
    assert "High-performance chip" in output

    assert "### PRICE" in output
    assert "Current price:" in output
    assert "Discount:" in output
    assert "Savings:" in output

    assert (
        "### WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU"
        in output
    )

    assert "### AVAILABLE FROM" in output
    assert "Demo Vendor" in output

    assert "### ACTIONS" in output
    assert "[GET DEAL]" in output
    assert "[MORE INFORMATION]" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Character notification
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "CHARACTER NOTIFICATION" in output
    assert "Yeti:" in output
    assert "Look what I found!" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Commerce boundary
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "STEP 5 — COMMERCE ACTION" in output
    assert "Commerce action: INITIATE_PURCHASE" in output
    assert "Status: REDIRECT" in output
    assert "Approved commerce boundary" in output
    assert "Vendor handoff" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Payment boundary
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final success
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS" in output

    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> "
        "Evaluation -> Personalised Discovery"
        in output
    )


def test_demo_preserves_correct_execution_order():

    result = run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    governance = output.index(
        "STEP 1 — GOVERNANCE"
    )

    intelligence = output.index(
        "STEP 2 — LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
    )

    evaluation = output.index(
        "STEP 3 — AI QUALITY EVALUATION"
    )

    discovery = output.index(
        "STEP 4 — PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
    )

    notification = output.index(
        "CHARACTER NOTIFICATION"
    )

    commerce = output.index(
        "STEP 5 — COMMERCE ACTION"
    )

    success = output.index(
        "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS"
    )

    assert governance < intelligence
    assert intelligence < evaluation
    assert evaluation < discovery
    assert discovery < notification
    assert notification < commerce
    assert commerce < success

"""
Reliability tests for the unified EasyChatBot hackathon demo.

H4.3 — Demo Reliability

These tests verify that the hackathon demonstration:

- starts successfully
- completes within a reasonable time
- does not require external API credentials
- produces the expected final success marker

The test intentionally executes the demo as a separate process,
matching how the hackathon presentation will actually run it.

It does not modify or bypass H1, H2, or H3 validation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_demo.py"
)


def _run_demo() -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    """
    Execute the unified demo with a bounded runtime.

    A timeout protects CI and the hackathon environment from
    a demo process hanging indefinitely.
    """

    return subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
        timeout=30,
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_completes_within_timeout():
    """
    Verify that the demo completes within 30 seconds.
    """

    result = _run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Hackathon demo did not complete successfully.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_is_self_contained():
    """
    Verify that the deterministic hackathon demonstration
    reaches its expected final state.

    The demo uses controlled providers and therefore should
    not require Parallel, LLM, or other external credentials.
    """

    result = _run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    assert (
        "Governance check: PASSED"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "Overall Score: 1.00"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "Status: PASS"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        in result.stdout
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_script_exists():

    assert DEMO_PATH.exists()
    assert DEMO_PATH.is_file()

"""
End-to-end integration test for the hackathon discovery journey.

Flow:

User Memory
    ↓
Deal Discovery
    ↓
Personalisation
    ↓
Character FlashDealCard
    ↓
GET DEAL
    ↓
CommerceAction
    ↓
CommerceService
    ↓
Vendor handoff

The test validates the complete hackathon vertical slice
without executing a real purchase or external payment.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionStatus,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_deal,
    build_demo_flash_deal_card,
    build_demo_memory,
)


def test_hackathon_discovery_to_commerce_flow():
    """
    Validate the complete personalised discovery journey.
    """

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. USER MEMORY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    memory = build_demo_memory()

    assert memory.user_id == "demo-user"

    assert memory.content

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. DEAL DISCOVERY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    deal = build_demo_deal()

    assert deal.deal_id

    assert deal.product_name == "iPhone 17 Pro"

    assert deal.vendor == "Demo Vendor"

    assert deal.price == 999.99

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. PERSONALISED FLASH DEAL
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card(
        deal=deal,
    )

    assert card.deal_id == deal.deal_id

    assert card.character_id == "yeti"

    assert card.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert card.product_name == deal.product_name

    assert card.relevance_reason

    assert (
        "mobile photography"
        in card.relevance_reason
    )

    assert card.has_discount is True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. COMMERCE ACTION
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        card=card,
    )

    assert action.user_id == memory.user_id

    assert action.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        action.action_type
        == CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )

    assert (
        action.destination_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. CONTROLLED COMMERCE SERVICE
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert result.action_id == action.action_id

    assert result.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. FINAL SAFETY BOUNDARY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    # The hackathon demonstrates a vendor handoff.
    # It does not claim that a payment occurred.

    assert (
        result.message
        == (
            "Continue to the approved vendor "
            "to complete the purchase."
        )
    )


def test_hackathon_flow_preserves_layer_separation():
    """
    Verify that commerce consumes the discovery result rather
    than changing it.
    """

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    original = {
        "deal_id": card.deal_id,
        "product_name": card.product_name,
        "price": card.current_price,
        "vendor": card.vendor_name,
        "relevance": card.relevance_reason,
        "get_deal_url": card.get_deal_url,
    }

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=card,
    )

    CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert card.deal_id == original["deal_id"]

    assert (
        card.product_name
        == original["product_name"]
    )

    assert (
        card.current_price
        == original["price"]
    )

    assert (
        card.vendor_name
        == original["vendor"]
    )

    assert (
        card.relevance_reason
        == original["relevance"]
    )

    assert (
        card.get_deal_url
        == original["get_deal_url"]
    )

"""
Acceptance tests for the Windows-safe judge-facing EasyChatBot demo.

The judge demo now includes the Payment Pass contract. The commerce

    ->
Payment Pass

READY / DEMO
    ->
No payment executed

import subprocess

def run_judge_demo() -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    """Execute the real judge-facing demonstration."""

    return subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,

def test_judge_demo_succeeds():

def test_judge_demo_preserves_execution_order():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    governance = output.index(
        "1 - GOVERNANCE"
    )

    intelligence = output.index(
        "2 - LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
    )

    learning = output.index(
        "3 - LEARNING"
    )

    evaluation = output.index(
        "4 - AI QUALITY EVALUATION"
    )

    discovery = output.index(
        "5 - PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
    )

    character = output.index(
        "6 - CHARACTER DISCOVERY"
    )

    deal = output.index(
        "7 - FLASH DEAL"
    )

    commerce = output.index(
        "8 - COMMERCE BOUNDARY"
    )

    payment = output.index(
        "PAYMENT PASS"
    )

    success = output.index(
        "JUDGE DEMO: SUCCESS"
    )

    assert governance < intelligence
    assert intelligence < learning
    assert learning < evaluation
    assert evaluation < discovery
    assert discovery < character
    assert character < deal
    assert deal < commerce
    assert commerce < payment
    assert payment < success

"""
Acceptance test for the character-driven discovery notification
inside the unified hackathon demonstration.

This deliberately executes the real demo as a subprocess.

Expected journey:

Governance
    ↓
Live Intelligence
    ↓
Learning
    ↓
Evaluation
    ↓
Personalised Discovery
    ↓
Character Notification
    ↓
Commerce Action
    ↓
Vendor Handoff
    ↓
Success
"""

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_demo.py"
)


def test_hackathon_demo_contains_character_notification():

    result = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Hackathon demo failed.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )

    output = result.stdout

    assert (
        "STEP 4 — PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "CHARACTER NOTIFICATION"
        in output
    )

    assert "Yeti:" in output

    assert (
        "Look what I found!"
        in output
    )

    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output

    assert (
        "Why this may interest you:"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "STEP 5 — COMMERCE ACTION"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Commerce action: INITIATE_PURCHASE"
        in output
    )

    assert "Status: REDIRECT" in output

    assert (
        "Approved commerce boundary"
        in output
    )

    assert "Vendor handoff" in output

    assert (
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS"
        in output
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_preserves_readiness_marker():

    result = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )

    assert result.returncode == 0

    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        in result.stdout
    )


===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_hackathon_payment_pass_demo.py

"""
Integration acceptance tests for the hackathon Payment Pass.

The test verifies the complete safe commerce path:

FlashDealCard
    ↓
FlashDealCommerce
    ↓
PaymentPass
    ↓
CommerceService
    ↓
READY / DEMO
    ↓
Vendor checkout

No real payment is executed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.payment_pass import (
    PaymentExecutionMode,
    PaymentPassStatus,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)


def make_card() -> FlashDealCard:
    """Create deterministic hackathon deal data."""

    return FlashDealCard(
        deal_id="deal-payment-demo-001",
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message=(
            "Look what I found! "
            "This could be perfect for you."
        ),
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
        current_price=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=1199.00,
        discount_percentage=17.0,
        savings=199.01,
        relevance_reason=(
            "Matches the user's preference for "
            "powerful mobile photography devices."
        ),
        vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
        more_information_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
        get_deal_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
    )


def test_complete_payment_pass_flow():

    card = make_card()

    commerce = FlashDealCommerce()

    payment_pass = commerce.create_payment_pass(
        user_id="user-001",
        card=card,
    )

    prepared = CommerceService().prepare_payment(
        payment_pass
    )

    assert prepared is payment_pass

    assert prepared.deal_id == card.deal_id
    assert prepared.user_id == "user-001"

    assert (
        prepared.product_name
        == card.product_name
    )

    assert (
        prepared.vendor_name
        == card.vendor_name
    )

    assert (
        prepared.amount
        == card.current_price
    )

    assert (
        prepared.currency
        == card.currency
    )

    assert (
        prepared.checkout_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    assert (
        prepared.status
        is PaymentPassStatus.READY
    )

    assert (
        prepared.execution_mode
        is PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
    )

    assert prepared.payment_executed is False


def test_payment_pass_cannot_become_executed():

    card = make_card()

    payment_pass = (
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="user-001",
            card=card,
        )
    )

    assert payment_pass.payment_executed is False

    assert (
        payment_pass.status
        is PaymentPassStatus.READY
    )

    assert (
        payment_pass.execution_mode
        is PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
    )


def test_payment_pass_preserves_vendor_checkout():

    card = make_card()

    payment_pass = (
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="user-001",
            card=card,
        )
    )

    prepared = CommerceService().prepare_payment(
        payment_pass
    )

    assert (
        prepared.checkout_url
        == "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
    )


def test_payment_pass_demo_render_is_explicit():

    card = make_card()

    payment_pass = (
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="user-001",
            card=card,
        )
    )

    prepared = CommerceService().prepare_payment(
        payment_pass
    )

    output = prepared.render_demo()

    assert "PAYMENT PASS" in output
    assert "Payment status: READY" in output
    assert "Execution mode: DEMO" in output
    assert "No payment has been executed." in output
    assert (
        "Vendor checkout remains outside "
        "the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )

"""
Final hackathon readiness checks for EasyChatBot.

H4.4 — Hackathon Readiness

This test verifies that the core hackathon deliverables exist
and that the repository contains the executable demonstrations
for the completed workstreams.

It intentionally does not implement new business logic.

Validated capabilities:

H1 — Live Intelligence
H2 — AI Quality Evaluation
H3 — AI Safety & Governance
H4 — Unified Hackathon Demonstration

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

    # H1 — Live Intelligence

    "src/providers/search_provider/interface.py",

)


def test_required_hackathon_files_exist():
    """

        for path in REQUIRED_FILES

        ).is_file()

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_hackathon_runtime_flow.py

"""
Hackathon Vertical Slice Runtime Integration Test.

WS-2 Sprint H2

Validates the application boundary only.

This test does not test:

- Runtime capability implementation
- Providers
- Agents

Those belong to other workstreams.

It validates:

API/Application boundary
        |
        v
Runtime Application Service
        |
        v
External Runtime Service
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock
from unittest.mock import Mock

from backend.services.external_runtime_service import (
    ExternalRuntimeService,
)

from backend.services.runtime.runtime_application_service import (
    RuntimeApplicationService,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_hackathon_vertical_slice_application_flow():
    """
    Validate the application runtime flow.

    Expected:

        User request
            |
            v
        RuntimeApplicationService
            |
            v
        ExternalRuntimeService
            |
            v
        Capability Result
    """

    expected_result = {
        "request_id": "hackathon-test-001",
        "capability": "live_intelligence",
        "status": "completed",
        "output": {
            "lesson": (
                "French lesson generated"
            )
        },
    }

    runtime_service = Mock()

    runtime_service.execute = AsyncMock(
        return_value=expected_result,
    )

    application_service = (
        RuntimeApplicationService(
            runtime_service=runtime_service,
        )
    )

    result = await application_service.execute(
        capability="live_intelligence",
        payload={
            "query": (
                "Teach French using today's AI news"
            )
        },
    )

    assert result == expected_result

    runtime_service.execute.assert_called_once()


===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_listening_proposal_capability.py
===============================================================================
"""
Integration tests for the Podcast Listening Proposal capability.

Flow:

PodcastRecommendation
        |
        v
CapabilityRequest
        |
        v
CapabilityGateway
        |
        v
ListeningProposalExecutor
        |
        v
ListeningProposal

The integration verifies that the capability executes
through the AI Runtime Gateway and remains passive.

No external provider is contacted.
No playback is started.
No external account is accessed.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.gateway import (
    CapabilityGateway,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.listening_proposal_executor import (
    ListeningProposalExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.listening import (
    ListeningProposal,
    ListeningProposalAction,
)

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    PodcastProviderName,
    PodcastRecommendation,
    PodcastSearchResult,
)


class FakeGovernanceResult:
    """Deterministic governance result for integration tests."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed


class FakeGovernance:
    """Deterministic governance implementation."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed
        self.calls = 0

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> FakeGovernanceResult:
        self.calls += 1

        return FakeGovernanceResult(
            allowed=self.allowed,
        )


def make_recommendation() -> PodcastRecommendation:
    """
    Create a deterministic PodcastRecommendation using
    the established repository contract.
    """

    result = PodcastSearchResult(
        provider=PodcastProviderName.APPLE,
        external_id="podcast-gateway-001",
        title="L'IA en Europe",
        description=(
            "Artificial intelligence and "
            "European regulation."
        ),
        publisher="Demo Publisher",
        languages=("fr",),
        url=(
            "https://example.com/"
            "podcasts/ia-en-europe"
        ),
        metadata={},
    )

    return PodcastRecommendation(
        recommendation_id="recommendation-gateway-001",
        user_id="user-001",
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message=(
            "I found a podcast for you."
        ),
        result=result,
        relevance_reason=(
            "This podcast matches your interests."
        ),
        match_score=92.0,
    )


def make_request(
    action: str | None = None,
) -> CapabilityRequest:
    """
    Create a Runtime Listening Proposal request.
    """

    payload = {
        "recommendation": make_recommendation(),
        "target_language": "fr",
        "learner_level": "B2",
    }

    if action is not None:
        payload["action"] = action

    return CapabilityRequest(
        capability=(
            CapabilityType.PODCAST_LISTENING_PROPOSAL
        ),
        request_id="listening-gateway-001",
        payload=payload,
        requested_by="learning_application",
    )


def make_gateway(
    governance: FakeGovernance,
) -> CapabilityGateway:
    """
    Build a Runtime Gateway containing the real
    ListeningProposalExecutor.
    """

    executor = ListeningProposalExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            executor,
        ],
    )

    return CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_listening_proposal_executes_through_gateway():
    """
    Verify successful Runtime Gateway execution.
    """

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True,
    )

    gateway = make_gateway(
        governance,
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        make_request(),
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert result.succeeded is True

    assert result.capability == (
        CapabilityType.PODCAST_LISTENING_PROPOSAL
    )

    assert result.request_id == (
        "listening-gateway-001"
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result.output,
        ListeningProposal,
    )

    assert governance.calls == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_returns_correct_listening_proposal():
    """
    Verify that the Gateway returns the expected
    provider-independent ListeningProposal.
    """

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True,
    )

    gateway = make_gateway(
        governance,
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        make_request(),
    )

    proposal = result.output

    assert isinstance(
        proposal,
        ListeningProposal,
    )

    assert proposal.user_id == "user-001"

    assert proposal.podcast_id == (
        "podcast-gateway-001"
    )

    assert proposal.podcast_title == (
        "L'IA en Europe"
    )

    assert proposal.character_id == "yeti"

    assert proposal.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert proposal.target_language == "fr"

    assert proposal.learner_level == "B2"

    assert proposal.action == (
        ListeningProposalAction.LISTEN
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_preserves_passive_security_boundary():
    """
    Verify that Runtime execution produces only a passive
    proposal and does not introduce autonomous action.
    """

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True,
    )

    gateway = make_gateway(
        governance,
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        make_request(),
    )

    proposal = result.output

    assert isinstance(
        proposal,
        ListeningProposal,
    )

    assert proposal.is_passive is True

    assert result.metadata["passive"] is True


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_supports_explicit_open_action():
    """
    Verify that the application may explicitly request
    the OPEN proposal action.
    """

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True,
    )

    gateway = make_gateway(
        governance,
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        make_request(
            action="open",
        ),
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    proposal = result.output

    assert isinstance(
        proposal,
        ListeningProposal,
    )

    assert proposal.action == (
        ListeningProposalAction.OPEN
    )

    assert proposal.is_passive is True


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_governance_rejection_prevents_listening_proposal_execution():
    """
    Verify governance remains the control boundary.

    A rejected capability must not execute the
    ListeningProposalExecutor.
    """

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=False,
    )

    gateway = make_gateway(
        governance,
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        make_request(),
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED
    )

    assert result.rejected is True

    assert result.output is None

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert result.metadata[
        "governance_decision"
    ] == "rejected"

            "I found a podcast for you."
        ),
        result=result,
        relevance_reason=(
            "This podcast matches your interests."
        ),
        match_score=92.0,
    )


def make_request() -> CapabilityRequest:
    """
    Create a valid Listening Proposal request.
    """

    return CapabilityRequest(
        capability=(
            CapabilityType.PODCAST_LISTENING_PROPOSAL
        ),
        request_id="registry-gateway-request-001",
        payload={
            "recommendation": make_recommendation(),
            "target_language": "fr",
            "learner_level": "B2",
        },
        requested_by="learning_application",
    )


def make_gateway(
    governance: FakeGovernance,
) -> CapabilityGateway:
    """
    Build the normal Registry -> Gateway Runtime path.
    """

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            ListeningProposalExecutor(),
        ],
    )

    return CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_registry_resolves_executor_for_gateway_execution():
    """
    Verify that the registry resolves the Listening Proposal
    executor used by the Runtime Gateway.
    """

    executor = ListeningProposalExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            executor,
        ],
    )

    resolved = registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.PODCAST_LISTENING_PROPOSAL,
    )

    assert resolved is executor


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_executes_registered_listening_capability():
    """
    Verify successful execution through the complete
    Registry -> Gateway -> Executor path.
    """

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True,
    )

    gateway = make_gateway(
        governance,
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        make_request(),
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert result.succeeded is True

    assert result.capability == (
        CapabilityType.PODCAST_LISTENING_PROPOSAL
    )

    assert result.request_id == (
        "registry-gateway-request-001"
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result.output,
        ListeningProposal,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_uses_governance_before_registered_executor():
    """
    Verify that governance remains before capability
    execution in the Registry -> Gateway path.
    """

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=False,
    )

    gateway = make_gateway(
        governance,
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        make_request(),
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED
    )

    assert result.rejected is True

    assert result.output is None

    assert governance.calls == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_registered_capability_remains_passive():
    """
    Verify that registry wiring does not change the
    passive nature of the Listening Proposal.
    """

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True,
    )

    gateway = make_gateway(
        governance,
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        make_request(),
    )

    proposal = result.output

    assert isinstance(
        proposal,
        ListeningProposal,
    )

    assert proposal.is_passive is True

    assert result.metadata["passive"] is True


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_registry_gateway_preserves_request_identity():
    """
    Verify Runtime correlation identity is preserved.
    """

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True,
    )

    gateway = make_gateway(
        governance,
    )

    request = make_request()

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request,
    )

    assert result.request_id == (
        request.request_id
    )

    assert result.capability == (
        request.capability
    )

"""
Integration test for H2 evaluation of the H1 learning challenge.

Flow:

H1 Live Intelligence / Challenge Engine
        ↓
LearningChallenge
        ↓
H2 DeterministicEvaluator
        ↓
CriterionScore[]
        ↓
EvaluationEngine
        ↓
EvaluationResult

This test intentionally uses deterministic test doubles.

It does not:
- call the Parallel API
- call an LLM
- modify H1 components
- use the Conversation Agent
"""

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    LearningChallenge,
    VocabularyItem,
)

from src.evaluation.criteria import (
    COMPLETENESS,
    EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
    LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
    SOURCE_COVERAGE,
)

from src.evaluation.deterministic_evaluator import (
    DeterministicEvaluator,
)

from src.evaluation.engine import (
    EvaluationEngine,
)


def _create_h1_learning_challenge() -> LearningChallenge:
    """
    Create a representative H1 learning challenge.

    This mirrors the structure produced by the H1
    Challenge Engine without calling external services.
    """

    return LearningChallenge(
        title="AI Act Learning Challenge",
        topic="AI Act",
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
        summary=(
            "The European Union AI Act establishes "
            "a risk-based framework for artificial "
            "intelligence."
        ),
        vocabulary=[
            VocabularyItem(
                term="gouvernance",
                definition=(
                    "La manière dont une organisation "
                    "est dirigée et contrôlée."
                ),
                example=(
                    "La gouvernance de l'IA devient "
                    "essentielle."
                ),
            ),
            VocabularyItem(
                term="réglementation",
                definition=(
                    "Ensemble des règles qui encadrent "
                    "une activité."
                ),
                example=(
                    "La réglementation européenne évolue."
                ),
            ),
        ],
        discussion_questions=[
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la réglementation de "
                    "l'IA est-elle importante ?"
                ),
                difficulty="B2",
            ),
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Quels sont les avantages d'une "
                    "réglementation européenne de l'IA ?"
                ),
                difficulty="B2",
            ),
        ],
    )


def test_h1_learning_challenge_can_be_evaluated():
    """
    Validate the complete H1 → H2 evaluation boundary.
    """

    challenge = _create_h1_learning_challenge()

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    assert len(scores) == 4

    score_names = {
        score.criterion
        for score in scores
    }

    assert score_names == {
        "completeness",
        "language_level",
        "educational_value",
        "source_coverage",
    }


def test_h1_learning_challenge_produces_quality_result():
    """
    Evaluate the H1 challenge through the H2 scoring engine.
    """

    challenge = _create_h1_learning_challenge()

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    engine = EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            COMPLETENESS,
            LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
            EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
            SOURCE_COVERAGE,
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )

    result = engine.evaluate(
        scores
    )

    assert result.overall_score == 1.0

    assert result.passed is True

    assert len(result.scores) == 4

    assert result.evaluator == "deterministic"


def test_h1_learning_challenge_contains_high_quality_components():
    """
    Verify the H1 challenge satisfies the basic quality
    dimensions currently supported by deterministic evaluation.
    """

    challenge = _create_h1_learning_challenge()

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    for score in scores:
        assert 0.0 <= score.score <= 1.0
        assert score.reasoning.strip()

    assert all(
        score.score == 1.0
        for score in scores
    )
