"""
End-to-end integration test for the Podcast learning flow.

Complete flow:

Podcast Discovery
    |
    v
PodcastDiscoveryExecutor
    |
    v
CapabilityGateway
    |
    v
PodcastRecommendation
    |
    v
Learning Generation
    |
    v
PodcastLearningChallengeExecutor
    |
    v
PodcastLearningChallenge

The test verifies that the output of the Podcast Discovery
capability can be used directly as the input to the Learning
Generation capability.

No external podcast provider is contacted.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityExecutionError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.gateway import (
    CapabilityGateway,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.podcast_discovery_executor import (
    PodcastDiscoveryExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.podcast_learning_challenge_executor import (
    PodcastLearningChallengeExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.agent import (
    PodcastDiscoveryAgent,
)

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.challenge import (
    PodcastLearningChallenge,
)

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    PodcastProviderName,
    PodcastRecommendation,
    PodcastSearchResult,
)


class FakeGovernanceResult:
    """
    Minimal governance result used by the integration test.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed


class FakeGovernance:
    """
    Deterministic governance implementation.

    Every capability execution must pass through governance.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed
        self.calls = 0

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> FakeGovernanceResult:
        self.calls += 1

        return FakeGovernanceResult(
            allowed=self.allowed,
        )


class FakePodcastProvider:
    """
    Deterministic podcast provider.

    This prevents any external Apple or Spotify API call.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0

    def search(
        self,
        intent,
    ) -> list[PodcastSearchResult]:
        self.calls += 1

        return [
            PodcastSearchResult(
                provider=PodcastProviderName.APPLE,
                external_id="podcast-flow-001",
                title="L'IA en Europe",
                description=(
                    "Artificial intelligence and "
                    "European regulation in French."
                ),
                publisher="Demo Publisher",
                languages=("fr",),
                url=(
                    "https://example.com/"
                    "podcasts/ia-en-europe"
                ),
                metadata={
                    "learner_level": "B2",
                },
            ),
        ]


def make_memory() -> MemoryEntry:
    """
    Create explicit learner memory for discovery.
    """

    return MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content=(
            "Interested in artificial intelligence "
            "and European regulation."
        ),
        metadata={
            "topics": (
                "artificial intelligence, "
                "European regulation"
            ),
        },
    )


def make_discovery_request() -> CapabilityRequest:
    """
    Build the Podcast Discovery Runtime request.
    """

    return CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.PODCAST_DISCOVERY,
        request_id="podcast-flow-discovery-001",
        payload={
            "user_id": "user-001",
            "world_id": "French World",
            "target_language": "fr",
            "learner_level": "B2",
            "learning_goal": (
                "Learn French through current affairs."
            ),
            "country_code": "LU",
            "memories": [
                make_memory(),
            ],
            "character_id": "yeti",
            "character_name": "Yeti",
            "character_message": (
                "I found this podcast for you!"
            ),
            "max_results": 10,
        },
        requested_by="learning_application",
    )


def make_learning_request(
    recommendation: PodcastRecommendation,
) -> CapabilityRequest:
    """
    Build the Learning Generation request using the
    actual output of Podcast Discovery.
    """

    return CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION,
        request_id="podcast-flow-learning-001",
        payload={
            "recommendation": recommendation,
        },
        requested_by="learning_application",
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_podcast_discovery_output_feeds_learning_generation():
    """
    Verify the complete Discovery → Recommendation →
    Learning Challenge flow.
    """

    provider = FakePodcastProvider()

    discovery_executor = PodcastDiscoveryExecutor(
        agent=PodcastDiscoveryAgent(),
        providers=[
            provider,
        ],
    )

    discovery_registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            discovery_executor,
        ],
    )

    discovery_governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True,
    )

    discovery_gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=discovery_registry,
        governance=discovery_governance,
    )

    discovery_result = (
        await discovery_gateway.execute(
            make_discovery_request(),
        )
    )

    assert (
        discovery_result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert discovery_result.succeeded is True

    assert discovery_result.capability == (
        CapabilityType.PODCAST_DISCOVERY
    )

    assert discovery_result.output is not None

    recommendation = discovery_result.output

    assert isinstance(
        recommendation,
        PodcastRecommendation,
    )

    assert recommendation.user_id == (
        "user-001"
    )

    assert recommendation.character_id == (
        "yeti"
    )

    assert recommendation.character_name == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    assert recommendation.result.external_id == (
        "podcast-flow-001"
    )

    assert recommendation.result.title == (
        "L'IA en Europe"
    )

    assert provider.calls == 1

    assert discovery_governance.calls == 1

    learning_executor = (
        PodcastLearningChallengeExecutor()
    )

    learning_registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            learning_executor,
        ],
    )

    learning_governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True,
    )

    learning_gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=learning_registry,
        governance=learning_governance,
    )

    learning_result = (
        await learning_gateway.execute(
            make_learning_request(
                recommendation,
            ),
        )
    )

    assert (
        learning_result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert learning_result.succeeded is True

    assert learning_result.capability == (
        CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION
    )

    assert isinstance(
        learning_result.output,
        PodcastLearningChallenge,
    )

    challenge = learning_result.output

    assert challenge.user_id == (
        recommendation.user_id
    )

    assert challenge.podcast_id == (
        recommendation.result.external_id
    )

    assert challenge.podcast_title == (
        recommendation.result.title
    )

    assert challenge.target_language == (
        "fr"
    )

    assert challenge.learner_level == (
        "B2"
    )

    assert challenge.vocabulary

    assert challenge.comprehension_questions

    assert challenge.discussion_prompt

    assert (
        challenge.metadata["content_basis"]
        == "verified_podcast_metadata"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.metadata["challenge_type"]
        == "podcast_learning_mvp"
    )

    assert learning_governance.calls == 1

    assert provider.calls == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_learning_generation_consumes_discovery_result_without_new_search():
    """
    Verify that Learning Generation consumes the existing
    PodcastRecommendation and does not perform another
    podcast provider search.
    """

    provider = FakePodcastProvider()

    discovery_executor = PodcastDiscoveryExecutor(
        agent=PodcastDiscoveryAgent(),
        providers=[
            provider,
        ],
    )

    discovery_gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=CapabilityRegistry(
            executors=[
                discovery_executor,
            ],
        ),
        governance=FakeGovernance(
            allowed=True,
        ),
    )

    discovery_result = (
        await discovery_gateway.execute(
            make_discovery_request(),
        )
    )

    assert discovery_result.succeeded is True

    recommendation = discovery_result.output

    assert isinstance(
        recommendation,
        PodcastRecommendation,
    )

    provider_calls_after_discovery = (
        provider.calls
    )

    learning_executor = (
        PodcastLearningChallengeExecutor()
    )

    learning_gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=CapabilityRegistry(
            executors=[
                learning_executor,
            ],
        ),
        governance=FakeGovernance(
            allowed=True,
        ),
    )

    learning_result = (
        await learning_gateway.execute(
            make_learning_request(
                recommendation,
            ),
        )
    )

    assert learning_result.succeeded is True

    assert (
        provider.calls
        == provider_calls_after_discovery
    )

    assert isinstance(
        learning_result.output,
        PodcastLearningChallenge,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_failure_does_not_create_learning_challenge():
    """
    Verify that a rejected Podcast Discovery execution
    cannot produce a Learning Challenge.

    The Gateway wraps executor failures in
    CapabilityExecutionError, so the integration boundary
    asserts that public Runtime error contract.
    """

    discovery_provider = FakePodcastProvider()

    discovery_executor = PodcastDiscoveryExecutor(
        agent=PodcastDiscoveryAgent(),
        providers=[
            discovery_provider,
        ],
    )

    discovery_gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=CapabilityRegistry(
            executors=[
                discovery_executor,
            ],
        ),
        governance=FakeGovernance(
            allowed=False,
        ),
    )

    discovery_result = (
        await discovery_gateway.execute(
            make_discovery_request(),
        )
    )

    assert (
        discovery_result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED
    )

    assert discovery_result.rejected is True

    assert discovery_result.output is None

    learning_executor = (
        PodcastLearningChallengeExecutor()
    )

    learning_gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=CapabilityRegistry(
            executors=[
                learning_executor,
            ],
        ),
        governance=FakeGovernance(
            allowed=True,
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityExecutionError,
        match="Capability executor failed: learning_generation",
    ):
        await learning_gateway.execute(
            make_learning_request(
                discovery_result.output,
            ),
        )


===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_provider_health_to_escalation.py

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health vertical slice.

Validates the complete internal operational flow:

Health Observation
        |
        v
Provider Health Service
        |
        v
UNAVAILABLE
        |
        v
Provider Incident Service
        |
        v
Provider Incident
        |
        v
Health Escalation Service
        |
        v
Escalation Service
        |
        v
Escalation Repository

No external provider calls are executed.

No email is sent.

No Runtime routing is changed.

No autonomous operational action is performed.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_repository import (
    InMemoryEscalationRepository,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_service import (
    EscalationService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.models import (
    EscalationSeverity,
    EscalationType,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_escalation_service import (
    ProviderHealthEscalationService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthStatus,
    ProviderHealthTransition,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_service import (
    ProviderHealthService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_incident_service import (
    InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository,
    ProviderIncidentService,
)


def create_observation(
    *,
    observed_at: datetime,
    consecutive_failures: int,
) -> ProviderHealthObservation:
    """
    Create deterministic OpenAI health evidence.
    """

    return ProviderHealthObservation(
        provider="openai",
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        capability="conversation",
        status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
        observed_at=observed_at,
        latency_ms=5000,
        consecutive_failures=consecutive_failures,
        consecutive_successes=0,
        error_type="provider_unavailable",
        error_message="Provider health probe failed.",
    )


def test_provider_health_to_escalation_vertical_slice() -> None:
    """
    Validate provider failure detection through escalation
    persistence.
    """

    health_service = ProviderHealthService()

    incident_repository = (
        InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository()
    )

    incident_service = (
        ProviderIncidentService(
            repository=incident_repository
        )
    )

    escalation_repository = (
        InMemoryEscalationRepository()
    )

    escalation_service = (
        EscalationService(
            repository=escalation_repository
        )
    )

    health_escalation_service = (
        ProviderHealthEscalationService(
            escalation_service=escalation_service
        )
    )

    first_time = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        32,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    second_time = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        33,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    third_time = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        34,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    first_transition = health_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=first_time,
            consecutive_failures=1,
        )
    )

    second_transition = health_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=second_time,
            consecutive_failures=2,
        )
    )

    third_transition = health_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=third_time,
            consecutive_failures=3,
        )
    )

    assert (
        first_transition.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.DEGRADED
    )

    assert (
        second_transition.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.DEGRADED
    )

    assert (
        third_transition.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.UNAVAILABLE
    )

    incident = (
        incident_service.process_transition(
            third_transition
        )
    )

    assert incident is not None

    assert (
        incident.provider
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        incident.status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
    )

    assert incident.is_open is True

    assert (
        incident.started_at
        == third_time
    )

    assert (
        incident.affected_capabilities
        == ("conversation",)
    )

    escalation = (
        health_escalation_service
        .escalate_incident(
            incident=incident
        )
    )

    assert escalation is not None

    assert (
        escalation.escalation_type
        == EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
    )

    assert (
        escalation.severity
        == EscalationSeverity.HIGH
    )

    assert (
        escalation.provider
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        escalation.capability
        == "conversation"
    )

    assert (
        "currently unavailable"
        in escalation.message
    )

    assert (
        escalation.recommendation
        is not None
    )

    assert (
        "human"
        in escalation.recommendation.lower()
    )

    persisted_escalations = (
        escalation_service
        .list_escalations()
    )

    assert (
        len(persisted_escalations)
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        persisted_escalations[0]
        .escalation_id
        == escalation.escalation_id
    )


def test_provider_recovery_closes_incident() -> None:
    """
    Validate the recovery side of the vertical slice.

    Provider degraded:

        14:32

    Provider degraded:

        14:33

    Provider becomes unavailable:

        14:34

    Provider recovery:

        14:39

    Expected confirmed downtime:

        300 seconds

    The incident starts when the provider crosses the
    confirmed UNAVAILABLE threshold at 14:34.
    """

    health_service = ProviderHealthService()

    incident_repository = (
        InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository()
    )

    incident_service = (
        ProviderIncidentService(
            repository=incident_repository
        )
    )

    failure_times = [
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            32,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            33,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            34,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    ]

    failure_events = []

    for index, timestamp in enumerate(
        failure_times,
        start=1,
    ):
        failure_events.append(
            health_service.observe(
                create_observation(
                    observed_at=timestamp,
                    consecutive_failures=index,
                )
            )
        )

    incident = (
        incident_service.process_transition(
            failure_events[-1]
        )
    )

    assert incident is not None

    assert (
        incident.started_at
        == datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            34,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        )
    )

    recovery_first = health_service.observe(
        ProviderHealthObservation(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
            observed_at=datetime(
                2026,
                8,
                14,
                14,
                39,
                tzinfo=timezone.utc,
            ),
            latency_ms=700,
            consecutive_failures=0,
            consecutive_successes=1,
        )
    )

    assert (
        recovery_first.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.RECOVERED
    )

    recovered = (
        incident_service.process_transition(
            recovery_first
        )
    )

    assert recovered is not None

    assert recovered.is_open is False

    assert (
        recovered.status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY
    )

    assert (
        recovered.downtime_seconds
        == 300.0
    )

    assert (
        recovered.recovered_at
        == datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            39,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        )
    )


===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_provider_usage_vertical_slice.py
===============================================================================
"""
AI Runtime provider intelligence vertical slice.

Validates:

Capability Request
        |
        v
Runtime Execution
        |
        v
Usage Capture
        |
        v
Provider Analytics

No real external provider calls are executed.

This test validates the internal intelligence
boundary only.
"""

from decimal import Decimal

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.models import (
    ProviderUsageEvent,
)

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.repository import (
    InMemoryProviderUsageRepository,
)

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.service import (
    ProviderUsageService,
)


def test_provider_usage_vertical_slice() -> None:
    """
    Validate provider usage tracking lifecycle.

    The usage service is responsible for:

    - recording execution consumption;
    - aggregating provider information;
    - exposing analytics data.

    It does not:

    - call providers;
    - execute agents;
    - perform payments.

    Persistence is provided through the
    repository boundary.
    """

    usage_service = ProviderUsageService(
        repository=(
            InMemoryProviderUsageRepository()
        ),
    )

    event = ProviderUsageEvent(
        provider="openai",

        model="gpt-5-mini",

        capability="conversation",

        functionality="chat",

        agent="conversation_agent",

        input_tokens=1000,

        output_tokens=500,

        latency_ms=800,

        estimated_cost=Decimal(
            "0.010"
        ),

        quality_score=95.0,

        success=True,
    )

    usage_service.record(
        event
    )

    report = usage_service.summary()

    assert report.total_requests == 1

    assert report.providers == (
        "openai",
    )

    assert report.total_tokens == 1500

    assert report.successful_requests == 1

    assert report.failed_requests == 0

    assert report.total_cost == Decimal(
        "0.010"
    )


===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_real_llm_service_flow.py
===============================================================================
"""
ConversationAgent -> LLMService integration test.

Verifies that the ConversationAgent uses the application-level
LLMService boundary correctly.

No real provider API is called.

Architecture:

ExecutionContext
    |
    v
ConversationAgent
    |
    v
LLMService
    |
    v
Fake LLMProvider
    |
    v
ProviderResult
    |
    v
AgentResult
"""

import pytest

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)

from backend.services.llm_service import (
    LLMService,
)


class FakeLLMProvider:
    """
    Deterministic provider used to verify the complete
    ConversationAgent -> LLMService -> Provider flow.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.received_request = None

    async def generate(
        self,
        request,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        self.received_request = request

        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="integration_fake",
            output=(
                "Artificial intelligence is "
                "the field of building systems "
                "that perform tasks requiring "
                "intelligent behaviour."
            ),
        )


def create_character() -> CharacterProfile:
    """
    Create the approved test character.
    """

    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_uses_llm_service():
    """
    Verify the complete ConversationAgent -> LLMService
    -> provider execution path.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider()

    llm_service = LLMService(
        provider
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character(),
        llm_service=llm_service,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="integration-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "conversation_agent"
    )

    assert result.output["character"] == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    assert result.output["message"] == (
        "Artificial intelligence is "
        "the field of building systems "
        "that perform tasks requiring "
        "intelligent behaviour."
    )

    assert result.output["provider_id"] == (
        "integration_fake"
    )

    assert provider.received_request is not None

    assert (
        provider.received_request.user_prompt
        == "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert (
        provider.received_request.character_id
        == "yeti"
    )

    assert (
        provider.received_request.character_name
        == "Yeti"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_preserves_provider_failure():
    """
    Verify that provider failures travel through
    LLMService and are converted into a failed
    AgentResult.
    """

    class FailingProvider:
        async def generate(
            self,
            request,
        ) -> ProviderResult:
            return ProviderResult(
                success=False,
                provider_id="integration_fake",
                error="Simulated provider failure",
            )

    service = LLMService(
        FailingProvider()
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character(),
        llm_service=service,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="integration-user",
        request="Explain AI",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is False

    assert result.output["provider_id"] == (
        "integration_fake"
    )

    assert result.output["provider_error"] == (
        "Simulated provider failure"
    )

    assert (
        result.output["message"]
        == (
            "The conversation provider "
            "could not generate a response."
        )
    )

import pytest

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)


def test_intelligence_context_creation():
    source = IntelligenceSource(
        title="European Commission",
        url="https://example.com",
        source="European Commission",
        content="AI regulation information.",
    )

    context = IntelligenceContext(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        sources=(source,),
        summary="Current regulatory information.",
    )

    assert context.query == (
        "latest European AI regulation"
    )

    assert context.source_count == 1

    assert context.has_sources is True

    assert context.has_summary is True


def test_intelligence_context_is_immutable():
    context = IntelligenceContext(
        query="latest AI news",
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        AttributeError
    ):
        context.query = "changed"


def test_intelligence_context_rejects_empty_query():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Intelligence query cannot be empty",
    ):
        IntelligenceContext(
            query="",
        )


def test_intelligence_source_requires_title():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="title cannot be empty",
    ):
        IntelligenceSource(
            title="",
            url="https://example.com",
            source="example",
        )


def test_intelligence_context_to_context_text():
    source = IntelligenceSource(
        title="AI Regulation",
        url="https://example.com/ai",
        source="Example",
        content="European AI regulation information.",
    )

    context = IntelligenceContext(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        sources=(source,),
        summary="Regulatory developments.",
    )

    text = context.to_context_text()

    assert "LIVE INTELLIGENCE" in text

    assert (
        "latest European AI regulation"
        in text
    )

    assert "AI Regulation" in text

    assert "https://example.com/ai" in text

    assert "European AI regulation information." in text

import pytest

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
)

from backend.intelligence.research_service import (
    ResearchService,
)


class FakeResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=IntelligenceContext(
                query=context.request,
            ),
        )


class FailingResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=False,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=None,
            error="Research unavailable",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_service_returns_intelligence_context():

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FakeResearchAgent()
    )

    result = await service.research(
        user_id="demo-user",
        query="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    assert result.query == (
        "Latest European AI regulation"
    )

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FakeResearchAgent()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Research query cannot be empty",
    ):
        await service.research(
            user_id="demo-user",
            query="",
        )

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FailingResearchAgent()

    with pytest.raises(
        RuntimeError,
        match="Research unavailable",
    ):
        await service.research(
            user_id="demo-user",
            query="Latest European AI regulation",
        )

"""
Provider Delivery Control tests.

Validates the manual provider-management kill switch.

Tests:
- initial active state;
- manual pause;
- execution blocking;
- pause reason;
- resume;
- repeated pause/resume operations.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


import pytest


from provider_management.control.provider_delivery_control import (
    ProviderDeliveryControl,
)



def test_provider_control_starts_active() -> None:
    """
    Provider execution is allowed by default.
    """

    control = ProviderDeliveryControl()


    assert (
        control.is_paused()
        is False
    )


    assert (
        control.allow_execution()
        is True
    )


    status = (
        control.status()
    )


    assert (
        status["paused"]
        is False
    )


    assert (
        status["reason"]
        is None
    )


    assert (
        status["changed_at"]
        is None
    )



def test_provider_control_pause_blocks_execution() -> None:
    """
    Manual pause must immediately block provider execution.
    """

    control = ProviderDeliveryControl()


    control.pause(
        reason=(
            "External provider security incident"
        ),
    )


    assert (
        control.is_paused()
        is True
    )


    assert (
        control.allow_execution()
        is False
    )


    status = (
        control.status()
    )


    assert (
        status["paused"]
        is True
    )


    assert (
        status["reason"]
        == "External provider security incident"
    )


    assert (
        status["changed_at"]
        is not None
    )



def test_provider_control_resume_allows_execution() -> None:
    """
    Resume must restore provider execution.
    """

    control = ProviderDeliveryControl()


    control.pause(
        reason="Emergency provider stop",
    )


    assert (
        control.allow_execution()
        is False
    )


    control.resume()


    assert (
        control.is_paused()
        is False
    )


    assert (
        control.allow_execution()
        is True
    )


    status = (
        control.status()
    )


    assert (
        status["paused"]
        is False
    )


    assert (
        status["reason"]
        is None
    )


    assert (
        status["changed_at"]
        is not None
    )



def test_provider_control_requires_pause_reason() -> None:
    """
    A manual emergency stop must contain an operational reason.
    """

    control = ProviderDeliveryControl()


    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="pause reason is required",
    ):

        control.pause("")



def test_provider_control_rejects_whitespace_pause_reason() -> None:
    """
    Whitespace-only reasons are invalid.
    """

    control = ProviderDeliveryControl()


    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="pause reason is required",
    ):

        control.pause("   ")



def test_provider_control_strips_pause_reason() -> None:
    """
    Stored operational reason should be normalised.
    """

    control = ProviderDeliveryControl()


    control.pause(
        reason="  Provider incident  ",
    )


    status = (
        control.status()
    )


    assert (
        status["reason"]
        == "Provider incident"
    )


def test_provider_control_supports_repeated_operations() -> None:
    """
    The control can be stopped and resumed repeatedly.
    """

    control = ProviderDeliveryControl()


    control.pause(
        reason="First provider incident",
    )


    assert (
        control.allow_execution()
        is False
    )


    control.resume()


    assert (
        control.allow_execution()
        is True
    )


    control.pause(
        reason="Second provider incident",
    )


    assert (
        control.allow_execution()
        is False
    )


    status = (
        control.status()
    )


    assert (
        status["reason"]
        == "Second provider incident"
    )


def test_provider_control_resume_is_idempotent() -> None:
    """
    Calling resume while already active is safe.
    """

    control = ProviderDeliveryControl()


    control.resume()


    assert (
        control.is_paused()
        is False
    )


    assert (
        control.allow_execution()
        is True
    )

"""
Tests for OpenAIProvider.

These tests never call the real OpenAI API.

The OpenAI AsyncOpenAI client is injected with a fake client
so provider behaviour can be tested deterministically.
"""

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.providers.llm.openai_provider import (
    OpenAIProvider,
)


class FakeResponses:
    """Fake OpenAI Responses API."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        response=None,
        error=None,
    ):
        self.response = response
        self.error = error
        self.received_kwargs = None

    async def create(
        self,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        self.received_kwargs = kwargs

        if self.error is not None:
            raise self.error

        return self.response


class FakeOpenAIClient:
    """Fake AsyncOpenAI client."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        response=None,
        error=None,
    ):
        self.responses = FakeResponses(
            response=response,
            error=error,
        )


def create_request(
    *,
    context=None,
):
    """Create a valid conversation request."""

    return ConversationRequest(
        system_instruction=(
            "You are Yeti, a friendly AI companion."
        ),
        user_prompt=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence."
        ),
        conversation_context=context,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
    )


def test_openai_provider_initialises():
    """Provider should initialise with a valid model."""

    client = FakeOpenAIClient()

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    assert provider.provider_id == "openai"
    assert provider.model == "gpt-5-mini"


def test_openai_provider_rejects_empty_model():
    """Provider should reject an empty model identifier."""

    client = FakeOpenAIClient()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="OpenAI model cannot be empty",
    ):
        OpenAIProvider(
            model="",
            client=client,
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_generates_response():
    """Successful OpenAI response should become ProviderResult."""

    response = SimpleNamespace(
        output_text="Artificial intelligence is the field of AI.",
    )

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        response=response,
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        create_request(),
    )

    assert result.success is True
    assert result.provider_id == "openai"
    assert (
        result.output
        == "Artificial intelligence is the field of AI."
    )
    assert result.error is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_builds_correct_request():
    """Provider should correctly translate ConversationRequest."""

    response = SimpleNamespace(
        output_text="Test response.",
    )

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        response=response,
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    request = create_request(
        context=(
            "LIVE INTELLIGENCE\n"
            "Research query: AI regulation"
        ),
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        request,
    )

    assert result.success is True

    sent = (
        client.responses.received_kwargs
    )

    assert sent is not None

    assert sent["model"] == "gpt-5-mini"

    messages = sent["input"]

    assert messages[0] == {
        "role": "system",
        "content": (
            "You are Yeti, a friendly AI companion."
        ),
    }

    assert messages[1] == {
        "role": "user",
        "content": (
            "Additional context:\n"
            "LIVE INTELLIGENCE\n"
            "Research query: AI regulation"
        ),
    }

    assert messages[2] == {
        "role": "user",
        "content": (
            "Explain artificial intelligence."
        ),
    }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_without_context():
    """Provider should not create a context message when absent."""

    response = SimpleNamespace(
        output_text="Test response.",
    )

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        response=response,
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        create_request(),
    )

    assert result.success is True

    messages = (
        client.responses.received_kwargs["input"]
    )

    assert len(messages) == 2

    assert messages[0]["role"] == "system"
    assert messages[1]["role"] == "user"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_handles_api_error():
    """OpenAI exceptions should become failed ProviderResult."""

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        error=RuntimeError(
            "OpenAI service unavailable"
        ),
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        create_request(),
    )

    assert result.success is False
    assert result.provider_id == "openai"
    assert result.output is None
    assert (
        result.error
        == "OpenAI service unavailable"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_handles_empty_response():
    """Empty OpenAI output should produce a failed result."""

    response = SimpleNamespace(
        output_text="",
    )

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        response=response,
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        create_request(),
    )

    assert result.success is False
    assert result.provider_id == "openai"
    assert result.output is None
    assert (
        result.error
        == "OpenAI returned an empty response."
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_rejects_none_request():
    """Provider should reject a missing conversation request."""

    client = FakeOpenAIClient()

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Conversation request cannot be None",
    ):
        await provider.generate(None)

import pytest

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_client import (
    ParallelClientWrapper,
)


class FakeSearchResult:

    def model_dump(self):
        return {
            "results": [
                {
                    "title": "Example",
                    "url": "https://example.com",
                }
            ]
        }


class FakeParallelClient:

    def search(
        self,
        *,
        search_queries,
        mode,
        objective,
    ):
        assert len(search_queries) == 1
        assert mode == "advanced"
        assert objective == (
            "Latest artificial intelligence news"
        )

        return FakeSearchResult()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_client_normalizes_response():

    wrapper = object.__new__(
        ParallelClientWrapper
    )

    wrapper.api_key = "test-key"

    wrapper._client = FakeParallelClient()

    result = await wrapper.search(
        "Latest artificial intelligence news"
    )

    assert result["query"] == (
        "Latest artificial intelligence news"
    )

    assert result["results"] == {
        "results": [
            {
                "title": "Example",
                "url": "https://example.com",
            }
        ]
    }

"""
Parallel Provider Tests.

These tests validate the provider layer
without calling the external Parallel API.

The real API integration is demonstrated
through the hackathon demo flow.
"""

import pytest

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_provider import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class MockParallelClient:
    """
    Fake Parallel client for automated tests.
    """

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
    ):

        return {
            "query": query,
            "results": [
                {
                    "title": "Example source",
                    "url": "https://example.com",
                }
            ],
        }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_provider_returns_results(
    monkeypatch,
):

    provider = ParallelSearchProvider(
    client=MockParallelClient()
)

    provider.client = MockParallelClient()

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Latest AI news",
    )

    result = await provider.execute(
        context
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        ProviderResult,
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.provider_id == (
        "parallel_search"
    )

    assert result.output["query"] == (
        "Latest AI news"
    )

    assert len(
        result.output["results"]
    ) == 1

import pytest

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_registry import (
    CharacterRegistry,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_clara_uses_clara_profile():

    registry = CharacterRegistry()

    clara = registry.get(
        "clara"
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        clara
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.output["character"] == (
        "Clara"
    )

    profile = result.output[
        "character_profile"
    ]

    assert profile["character_id"] == (
        "clara"
    )

    assert profile["name"] == (
        "Clara"
    )

    assert profile["description"] == (
        "A professional learning companion "
        "focused on technology and career topics."
    )

    assert profile["personality"] == (
        "Analytical, structured and supportive."
    )

    assert profile["learning_style"] == (
        "Structured and practical learning."
    )

def test_character_registry_returns_yeti():

    registry = CharacterRegistry()

    character = registry.get("yeti")

    assert character.character_id == "yeti"

    assert character.name == "Yeti"


def test_character_registry_returns_clara():

    registry = CharacterRegistry()

    character = registry.get("clara")

    assert character.character_id == "clara"

    assert character.name == "Clara"


def test_character_registry_is_case_insensitive():

    registry = CharacterRegistry()

    character = registry.get("CLARA")

    assert character.character_id == "clara"

    characters = registry.list_characters()

import pytest

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.memory.memory_service import (
    MemoryService,
)

from backend.memory.memory_store import (
    MemoryStore,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)

from backend.services.companion_service import (
    CompanionService,
)


class FakeConversationAgent:

    def __init__(self):
        self.received_context = None

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        self.received_context = context

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={
                "character": "Yeti",
                "personality": (
                    "Curious and encouraging"
                ),
                "learning_style": (
                    "Conversation based learning"
                ),
                "intelligence_context": (
                    context.intelligence_context
                ),
            },
        )


class FakeResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=IntelligenceContext(
                query=context.request,
                sources=(
                    IntelligenceSource(
                        title=(
                            "European AI Regulation"
                        ),
                        url=(
                            "https://example.com/ai"
                        ),
                        source="test",
                        content=(
                            "Current regulatory "
                            "information."
                        ),
                    ),
                ),
                summary=(
                    "Recent European AI "
                    "regulatory developments."
                ),
            ),
        )


class FakeQualityAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
                "findings": [],
                "recommendations": [],
            },
        )


def create_character():
    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


def create_memory_service(
    tmp_path,
):
    return MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            database_path=(
                tmp_path / "memory.db"
            )
        )
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_passes_research_to_conversation(
    tmp_path,
):

    conversation_agent = (
        FakeConversationAgent()
    )

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=(
            conversation_agent
        ),
        research_agent=(
            FakeResearchAgent()
        ),
        quality_agent=(
            FakeQualityAgent()
        ),
        memory_service=(
            create_memory_service(
                tmp_path
            )
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain the latest "
            "European AI regulation"
        ),
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=True,
    )

    assert result["character"] == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    assert (
        result["research"]
        is not None
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result["research"],
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    received_context = (
        conversation_agent.received_context
    )

    assert (
        received_context is not None
    )

    assert (
        received_context
        is not context
    )

    assert (
        received_context.intelligence_context
        is not None
    )

    assert (
        received_context.intelligence_context.query
        == context.request
    )

    assert (
        received_context.intelligence_context.source_count
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        result["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        is received_context.intelligence_context
    )

    assert (
        context.intelligence_context
        is None
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_without_research_preserves_normal_conversation(
    tmp_path,
):

    conversation_agent = (
        FakeConversationAgent()
    )

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=(
            conversation_agent
        ),
        research_agent=(
            FakeResearchAgent()
        ),
        quality_agent=(
            FakeQualityAgent()
        ),
        memory_service=(
            create_memory_service(
                tmp_path
            )
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=False,
    )

    assert result["research"] is None

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_context
        is context
    )

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_context
        .intelligence_context
        is None
    )

    assert (
        result["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        is None
    )

import pytest

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.memory.memory_service import (
    MemoryService,
)

from backend.memory.memory_store import (
    MemoryStore,
)

from backend.services.companion_service import (
    CompanionService,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_stores_memory(
    tmp_path,
):

    character = CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description="Learning companion",
        personality="Friendly",
        learning_style="Conversation",
    )

    memory_service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            database_path=(
                tmp_path / "memory.db"
            )
        )
    )

    service = CompanionService(
        character=character,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "I want to improve French speaking"
        ),
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        context
    )

    assert result["character"] == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    memories = (
        service.memory_service.recall(
            "demo-user"
        )
    )

    assert len(memories) == 1

    assert memories[0].content == (
        "I want to improve French speaking"
    )

    assert memories[0].category == (
        "conversation"
    )

import pytest

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


def create_character():
    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "conversation_agent"
    )

    assert result.output["character"] == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    assert result.output["personality"] == (
        "Curious and encouraging"
    )

    assert result.output["learning_style"] == (
        "Conversation based learning"
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result.output["conversation_request"],
        ConversationRequest,
    )

    request = (
        result.output["conversation_request"]
    )

    assert request.character_id == "yeti"

    assert request.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert request.user_prompt == (
        "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is False
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_includes_live_intelligence():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    intelligence = IntelligenceContext(
        query=(
            "Latest European AI regulation"
        ),
        sources=(
            IntelligenceSource(
                title="European AI Regulation",
                url="https://example.com/ai",
                source="parallel",
                content=(
                    "Current regulatory information."
                ),
            ),
        ),
        summary=(
            "Recent regulatory developments."
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain the latest European "
            "AI regulation"
        ),
        intelligence_context=intelligence,
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    request = (
        result.output["conversation_request"]
    )

    assert isinstance(
        request,
        ConversationRequest,
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is True
    )

    assert (
        "LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        "European AI Regulation"
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        "Current regulatory information."
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        result.output["intelligence_context"]
        == request.conversation_context
    )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\runtime\test_conversation_agent_provider.py

"""
Tests for ConversationAgent LLM provider integration.

These tests verify the architecture:

ConversationAgent
    |
    v
LLMService
    |
    v
Injected LLMProvider
    |
    v
ProviderResult

No real provider API is called.
"""

import pytest

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class FakeLLMProvider:
    """
    Deterministic provider for ConversationAgent tests.

    The provider is injected into ConversationAgent and
    therefore wrapped by LLMService internally.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        result: ProviderResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.received_request = None

    async def generate(
        self,
        request,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Capture the request and return the configured result.
        """

        self.received_request = request

        return self.result


def create_character() -> CharacterProfile:
    """
    Create the deterministic test character.
    """

    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_executes_injected_provider():
    """
    ConversationAgent should execute an injected provider
    through the LLMService boundary.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider(
        ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output=(
                "Artificial intelligence is "
                "the field of building systems "
                "that perform tasks requiring intelligence."
            ),
        )
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character(),
        llm_provider=provider,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain artificial intelligence",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.output["message"] == (
        "Artificial intelligence is "
        "the field of building systems "
        "that perform tasks requiring intelligence."
    )

    assert result.output["provider_id"] == (
        "fake_llm"
    )

    assert provider.received_request is not None

    assert (
        provider.received_request.user_prompt
        == "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert (
        provider.received_request.character_id
        == "yeti"
    )

    assert (
        agent.llm_service is not None
    )

    assert (
        agent.llm_service.llm_provider
        is provider
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_handles_provider_failure():
    """
    Provider failures should be converted into a failed
    AgentResult without exposing provider SDK details.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider(
        ProviderResult(
            success=False,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            error="Provider unavailable",
        )
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character(),
        llm_provider=provider,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain artificial intelligence",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is False

    assert result.output["provider_id"] == (
        "fake_llm"
    )

    assert result.output["provider_error"] == (
        "Provider unavailable"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_without_provider_keeps_demo_behaviour():
    """
    ConversationAgent should preserve deterministic
    hackathon behaviour when no LLM service/provider
    is configured.
    """

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain artificial intelligence",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert (
        result.output["message"]
        == (
            "Yeti: Let's explore this together. "
            "You asked: Explain artificial intelligence"
        )
    )

    assert (
        "provider_id"
        not in result.output
    )

    assert (
        agent.llm_service
        is None
    )


def test_conversation_agent_rejects_both_service_and_provider():
    """
    ConversationAgent should reject ambiguous dependency
    injection when both LLMService and LLMProvider are supplied.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider(
        ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output="Test response",
        )
    )

    from backend.services.llm_service import (
        LLMService,
    )

    service = LLMService(
        provider
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=(
            "Provide either llm_service or "
            "llm_provider, not both"
        ),
    ):
        ConversationAgent(
            character=create_character(),
            llm_service=service,
            llm_provider=provider,
        )

import pytest

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


def test_execution_context_creation():

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Hello",
    )

    assert context.user_id == (
        "demo-user"
    )

    assert context.request == (
        "Hello"
    )

    assert context.execution_id

    assert context.created_at

    assert context.metadata == {}

    assert context.intelligence_context is None

    assert context.has_intelligence_context is False


def test_execution_context_is_immutable():

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Hello",
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        AttributeError
    ):
        context.request = "Changed"


def test_execution_context_can_carry_intelligence():

    intelligence = IntelligenceContext(
        query="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain this to me",
        intelligence_context=intelligence,
    )

    assert context.intelligence_context is intelligence

    assert context.has_intelligence_context is True

    assert (
        context.intelligence_context.query
        == "Latest European AI regulation"
    )

"""
Tests External Runtime Container.

Validates:

Application Bootstrap
        |
        v
External Runtime Container
        |
        v
ExternalRuntimeService

The test uses controlled dependencies.

No external providers are called.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from backend.runtime.external_runtime_container import (
    configure_external_runtime_service,
    get_external_runtime_service,
)

from backend.services.external_runtime_service import (
    ExternalRuntimeService,
)


class FakeGateway:
    """
    Controlled Runtime gateway.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        request,
    ):
        return None


def test_container_registers_runtime_service():
    """
    Configured service should be retrievable.
    """

    service = ExternalRuntimeService(
        gateway=FakeGateway(),
    )

    configure_external_runtime_service(
        service,
    )

    result = get_external_runtime_service()

    assert result is service


def test_container_rejects_missing_service():
    """
    Container should reject invalid configuration.
    """

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="ExternalRuntimeService cannot be None",
    ):
        configure_external_runtime_service(
            None,
        )

from backend.memory.memory_service import (
    MemoryService,
)

from backend.memory.memory_store import (
    MemoryStore,

def test_memory_save_and_recall(
    tmp_path,
):

    database_path = (
        tmp_path / "memory.db"
    )

    service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            database_path=database_path
        )
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="demo-user",
        category="learning_goal",
        content=(
            "Improve French conversation skills"
        ),
    )

    memories = service.recall(
        "demo-user"
    )

    assert len(memories) == 1

    assert memories[0].user_id == (
        "demo-user"
    )

    assert memories[0].category == (
        "learning_goal"
    )

    assert memories[0].content == (
        "Improve French conversation skills"
    )

from backend.runtime.models.model_id import ModelId
from backend.runtime.models.model_capabilities import ModelCapabilities
from backend.runtime.models.model_descriptor import ModelDescriptor


def test_model_id():

    model = ModelId(
        provider="google",
        name="gemini-2.5-flash",
    )

    assert model.full_name() == (
        "google:gemini-2.5-flash"
    )


def test_model_descriptor():

    descriptor = ModelDescriptor(
        model_id=ModelId(
            provider="google",
            name="gemini-2.5-flash",
        ),
        capabilities=ModelCapabilities(
            reasoning=True,
            tool_usage=True,
        ),
        description="Fast reasoning model",
    )

    assert descriptor.model_id.provider == "google"

    assert descriptor.capabilities.reasoning is True

    assert descriptor.capabilities.tool_usage is True

import pytest

from backend.providers.base_provider import BaseProvider
from backend.providers.provider_result import ProviderResult
from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext


class DemoProvider(BaseProvider):

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ProviderResult:

        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id=self.provider_id,
            output="Provider response",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_provider_execution():

    provider = DemoProvider(
        provider_id="demo_provider"
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="user1",
        request="Generate response"
    )

    result = await provider.execute(context)

    assert result.success is True
    assert result.provider_id == "demo_provider"
    assert result.output == "Provider response"

@pytest.mark.asyncio

    tmp_path,
):

    character = CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description="Companion",
        personality="Friendly",
        learning_style="Conversation",
    )

    memory_service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            database_path=(
                tmp_path / "memory.db"
            )
        )
    )

    service = CompanionService(
        character=character,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        ExecutionContext(
            user_id="demo-user",
            request="Learn French",
        )
    )

    assert (
        result["quality"]["passed"]
        is True
    )

    assert (
        result["quality"]["quality_score"]
        > 0
    )

import pytest

from backend.agents.research_agent import (
    ResearchAgent,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class FakeResearchProvider:
    """
    Fake research provider used for unit testing.

    This keeps the test independent from the real
    Parallel Search API.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_research",
            output={
                "query": context.request,
                "results": {
                    "results": [
                        {
                            "title": "European AI Regulation",
                            "url": "https://example.com/ai",
                            "excerpts": [
                                "Current regulatory information."
                            ],
                        }
                    ]
                },
            },
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_agent_returns_intelligence_context():

    agent = ResearchAgent(
        provider=FakeResearchProvider()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "research_agent"
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result.output,
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    assert result.output.query == (
        "Latest European AI regulation"
    )

    assert result.output.source_count == 1

    assert isinstance(
        result.output.sources[0],
        IntelligenceSource,
    )

    assert result.output.sources[0].title == (
        "European AI Regulation"
    )

    assert result.output.sources[0].url == (
        "https://example.com/ai"
    )

    assert (
        result.output.sources[0].content
        == "Current regulatory information."
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_agent_propagates_provider_failure():

    class FailingResearchProvider:
        """
        Fake provider representing a provider failure.
        """

        async def execute(
            self,
            context,
        ):
            return ProviderResult(
                success=False,
                provider_id="fake_research",
                output=None,
                error="Research provider unavailable",
            )

    agent = ResearchAgent(
        provider=FailingResearchProvider()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is False

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "research_agent"
    )

    assert result.output is None

    assert result.error == (
        "Research provider unavailable"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_agent_handles_empty_results():

    class EmptyResearchProvider:
        """
        Fake provider returning no research results.
        """

        async def execute(
            self,
            context,
        ):
            return ProviderResult(
                success=True,
                provider_id="fake_research",
                output={
                    "query": context.request,
                    "results": {
                        "results": []
                    },
                },
            )

    agent = ResearchAgent(
        provider=EmptyResearchProvider()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="A very specific research question",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert isinstance(
        result.output,
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    assert result.output.source_count == 0

    assert result.output.has_sources is False
