"""
Unit tests for CompanionService.

These tests verify the application orchestration boundary:

ExecutionContext
|
v
CompanionService
|
+--> MemoryService
|
+--> ResearchAgent
|       |
|       v
|   IntelligenceContext
|
+--> ConversationAgent
|
+--> QualityAgent
|
v
Interaction Result

No real LLM provider, OpenAI API, Parallel API,
or external infrastructure is called.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass

import pytest

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)

from backend.services.companion_service import (
    CompanionService,
)


@dataclass
class FakeMemory:
    """
    Deterministic memory record for tests.
    """

    user_id: str
    category: str
    content: str


class FakeMemoryService:
    """
    Deterministic MemoryService test double.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.memories = [
            FakeMemory(
                user_id="demo-user",
                category="conversation",
                content="Previous conversation",
            ),
        ]

        self.recall_calls = []
        self.remember_calls = []

    def recall(
        self,
        user_id: str,
    ):
        self.recall_calls.append(
            user_id
        )

        return [
            memory
            for memory in self.memories
            if memory.user_id == user_id
        ]

    def remember(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        category: str,
        content: str,
    ) -> None:
        self.remember_calls.append(
            {
                "user_id": user_id,
                "category": category,
                "content": content,
            }
        )


class FakeResearchAgent:
    """
    Deterministic ResearchAgent test double.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        result: AgentResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.received_contexts = []

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        self.received_contexts.append(
            context
        )

        return self.result


class FakeConversationAgent:
    """
    Deterministic ConversationAgent test double.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        result: AgentResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.received_contexts = []

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        self.received_contexts.append(
            context
        )

        return self.result


class FakeQualityAgent:
    """
    Deterministic QualityAgent test double.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        result: AgentResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.received_contexts = []

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        self.received_contexts.append(
            context
        )

        return self.result


def create_character() -> CharacterProfile:
    """
    Create a deterministic test character.
    """

    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


def create_context(
    request: str = "Explain artificial intelligence",
) -> ExecutionContext:
    """
    Create a deterministic execution context.
    """

    return ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=request,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_service_orchestrates_conversation():
    """
    CompanionService should execute the ConversationAgent
    and QualityAgent for a normal interaction.

    Research must not execute when use_research=False.
    """

    conversation_agent = FakeConversationAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={
                "message": "Fake conversation response",
            },
        )
    )

    research_agent = FakeResearchAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output="Should not execute",
        )
    )

    quality_agent = FakeQualityAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
            },
        )
    )

    memory_service = FakeMemoryService()

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=conversation_agent,
        research_agent=research_agent,
        quality_agent=quality_agent,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    context = create_context()

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=False,
    )

    assert result["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert result["conversation"] == {
        "message": "Fake conversation response",
    }

    assert result["research"] is None

    assert result["quality"] == {
        "quality_score": 1.0,
        "passed": True,
    }

    assert result["memory_count"] == 1

    assert (
        len(
            conversation_agent.received_contexts
        )
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_contexts[0]
        is context
    )

    assert (
        len(
            research_agent.received_contexts
        )
        == 0
    )

    assert (
        len(
            quality_agent.received_contexts
        )
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        quality_agent.received_contexts[0]
        is context
    )

    assert memory_service.recall_calls == [
        "demo-user",
    ]

    assert memory_service.remember_calls == [
        {
            "user_id": "demo-user",
            "category": "conversation",
            "content": (
                "Explain artificial intelligence"
            ),
        }
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_service_executes_research_when_requested():
    """
    When research is requested, CompanionService should:

    1. execute ResearchAgent
    2. attach its IntelligenceContext to a new
       ExecutionContext
    3. execute ConversationAgent with the enriched context
    4. execute QualityAgent with the enriched context
    """

    intelligence_context = object()

    research_agent = FakeResearchAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=intelligence_context,
        )
    )

    conversation_agent = FakeConversationAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={
                "message": "Research-aware response",
            },
        )
    )

    quality_agent = FakeQualityAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={
                "quality_score": 0.95,
                "passed": True,
            },
        )
    )

    memory_service = FakeMemoryService()

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=conversation_agent,
        research_agent=research_agent,
        quality_agent=quality_agent,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    context = create_context(
        "What are the latest AI developments?"
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=True,
    )

    assert result["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert result["conversation"] == {
        "message": "Research-aware response",
    }

    assert (
        result["research"]
        is intelligence_context
    )

    assert result["quality"] == {
        "quality_score": 0.95,
        "passed": True,
    }

    assert (
        len(
            research_agent.received_contexts
        )
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        research_agent.received_contexts[0]
        is context
    )

    assert (
        len(
            conversation_agent.received_contexts
        )
        == 1
    )

    enriched_context = (
        conversation_agent.received_contexts[0]
    )

    assert enriched_context is not context

    assert (
        enriched_context.request
        == context.request
    )

    assert (
        enriched_context.user_id
        == context.user_id
    )

    assert (
        enriched_context.intelligence_context
        is intelligence_context
    )

    assert (
        quality_agent.received_contexts[0]
        is enriched_context
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_service_does_not_mutate_original_context():
    """
    Research enrichment must create a new
    ExecutionContext rather than mutating the original.
    """

    intelligence_context = object()

    research_agent = FakeResearchAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=intelligence_context,
        )
    )

    conversation_agent = FakeConversationAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={
                "message": "Response",
            },
        )
    )

    quality_agent = FakeQualityAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
            },
        )
    )

    memory_service = FakeMemoryService()

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=conversation_agent,
        research_agent=research_agent,
        quality_agent=quality_agent,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    context = create_context()

    assert (
        context.intelligence_context
        is None
    )

    await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=True,
    )

    assert (
        context.intelligence_context
        is None
    )

    enriched_context = (
        conversation_agent.received_contexts[0]
    )

    assert enriched_context is not context

    assert (
        enriched_context.intelligence_context
        is intelligence_context
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_service_handles_research_failure():
    """
    If research fails, ConversationAgent should still
    execute using the original context.

    The service should not attach failed research output
    to the conversation context.
    """

    research_agent = FakeResearchAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=False,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=None,
        )
    )

    conversation_agent = FakeConversationAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={
                "message": "Fallback response",
            },
        )
    )

    quality_agent = FakeQualityAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={
                "quality_score": 0.9,
                "passed": True,
            },
        )
    )

    memory_service = FakeMemoryService()

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=conversation_agent,
        research_agent=research_agent,
        quality_agent=quality_agent,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    context = create_context()

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=True,
    )

    assert result["research"] is None

    assert result["conversation"] == {
        "message": "Fallback response",
    }

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_contexts[0]
        is context
    )

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_contexts[0]
        .intelligence_context
        is None
    )


def test_companion_service_uses_injected_dependencies():
    """
    CompanionService should preserve explicitly injected
    dependencies rather than replacing them with defaults.
    """

    conversation_agent = FakeConversationAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={},
        )
    )

    research_agent = FakeResearchAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=None,
        )
    )

    quality_agent = FakeQualityAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={},
        )
    )

    memory_service = FakeMemoryService()

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=conversation_agent,
        research_agent=research_agent,
        quality_agent=quality_agent,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    assert (
        service.conversation_agent
        is conversation_agent
    )

    assert (
        service.research_agent
        is research_agent
    )

    assert (
        service.quality_agent
        is quality_agent
    )

    assert (
        service.memory_service
        is memory_service
    )


===============================================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\services\test_llm_service.py

"""
Unit tests for LLMService.

These tests verify that:

ConversationRequest
        |
        v
LLMService
        |
        v
Injected LLMProvider
        |
        v
ProviderResult

The tests never call a real provider API.
"""

import pytest

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.services.llm_service import (
    LLMService,
)


class FakeLLMProvider:
    """
    Deterministic LLM provider for service tests.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        result: ProviderResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.received_request = None

    async def generate(
        self,
        request: ConversationRequest,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        self.received_request = request

        return self.result


def create_request() -> ConversationRequest:
    """
    Create a valid provider-independent request.
    """

    return ConversationRequest(
        system_instruction=(
            "You are Yeti, a friendly AI companion."
        ),
        user_prompt=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence."
        ),
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
    )


def test_llm_service_rejects_none_provider():
    """
    LLMService must require a provider.
    """

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="LLM provider cannot be None",
    ):
        LLMService(
            None,
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_llm_service_delegates_to_provider():
    """
    LLMService should delegate the request to the
    injected provider.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider(
        ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output="Fake AI response",
        )
    )

    service = LLMService(
        provider,
    )

    request = create_request()

    result = await service.generate(
        request,
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.provider_id == (
        "fake_llm"
    )

    assert result.output == (
        "Fake AI response"
    )

    assert provider.received_request is request


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_llm_service_preserves_provider_failure():
    """
    Provider failures should be returned unchanged
    as ProviderResult.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider(
        ProviderResult(
            success=False,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            error="Provider unavailable",
        )
    )

    service = LLMService(
        provider,
    )

    result = await service.generate(
        create_request(),
    )

    assert result.success is False

    assert result.provider_id == (
        "fake_llm"
    )

    assert result.output is None

    assert result.error == (
        "Provider unavailable"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_llm_service_rejects_none_request():
    """
    LLMService should reject a missing request before
    invoking the provider.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider(
        ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output="Should not execute",
        )
    )

    service = LLMService(
        provider,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Conversation request cannot be None",
    ):
        await service.generate(
            None,
        )

    assert provider.received_request is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_llm_service_rejects_provider_without_result():
    """
    A provider returning None violates the service
    execution contract and should produce a RuntimeError.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider(
        None,
    )

    service = LLMService(
        provider,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        RuntimeError,
        match="LLM provider returned no result",
    ):
        await service.generate(
            create_request(),
        )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\services\test_runtime_application_service.py

"""
Tests for Runtime Application Service.

Validates:

Application Layer
        |
        v
RuntimeApplicationService
        |
        v
ExternalRuntimeService
        |
        v
Capability Result

The tests use controlled fakes.

They do not call:

- Parallel API;
- Gemini;
- Google Cloud;
- external providers.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from backend.services.runtime.runtime_application_service import (
    RuntimeApplicationService,
)

from backend.services.external_runtime_service import (
    ExternalRuntimeService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


class FakeRuntimeGateway:
    """
    Controlled Runtime gateway fake.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        request,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:
        """
        Return deterministic Runtime result.
        """

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={
                "result": "runtime-success",
            },
        )


def create_service() -> RuntimeApplicationService:
    """
    Create RuntimeApplicationService with fake Runtime.
    """

    runtime_service = ExternalRuntimeService(
        gateway=FakeRuntimeGateway(),
    )

    return RuntimeApplicationService(
        runtime_service=runtime_service,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_application_service_executes_capability():
    """
    RuntimeApplicationService should create a Runtime request
    and delegate execution.
    """

    service = create_service()

    result = await service.execute(
        capability="live_intelligence",
        payload={
            "query": "latest AI news",
        },
    )

    assert result.status == (
        CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert result.capability == (
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )

    assert result.output == {
        "result": "runtime-success",
    }

    assert result.request_id is not None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_application_service_requires_capability():
    """
    Empty capability should be rejected at the application
    boundary.
    """

    service = create_service()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Capability cannot be empty",
    ):
        await service.execute(
            capability="",
            payload={},
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_application_service_requires_payload():
    """
    Missing payload should be rejected.
    """

    service = create_service()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Payload cannot be None",
    ):
        await service.execute(
            capability="live_intelligence",
            payload=None,
        )
